# DESIGN_FP_ARCH_CH — THE CHAMPIONS: champions and villains, the most agentic persons in the world, introduced by the DM and answered by the world

## Compiled on the owner's word of 2026-10-17 in the FP final architecture program (kit `settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/`) and amended in place with THE PATRON DEITY on the owner's word of 2026-10-20; DARK behind six virtual flags (§19); nothing scheduled.
## CONSOLIDATED 2026-10-20 from the bracketed text of record at `1beabdc85` — the review trail is in git and in `settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-cure-record.md`; the compile log and its measurements (taken at master `81f1cd963`) are the kit's `cure/L-CH.md`.
## Bound by the FP constitution's requirements 1–24 (`DESIGN_FP_SPINE.md` §1; 15–24's text of record is `DESIGN_FP_COHERENCE_FOLD.md` §1) and by the coherence fold. The OWNER's shapes and rows are marked ⭐; every ruling is vetoable (§23).

---

## THE READER'S GUIDE

1. WHAT THIS IS. A champion or a villain — code token **`paragon`** (`champion` is landed); prose words champion, villain and the sixteen display nouns — is a person the DM alone introduces (CH1), carried as ONE conditional mark on the world-NPC ledger (CH2), who each week CHOOSES whether to stay or go and whether to use its gift where it stands (§4), and whose gift leans ONE landed seam by a bounded amount, its villain leaning the same seam the other way (CH5, CH6, §6). It may be bound to a court or free (§7), barred at a town's gate (§11), sworn to a god (§10b), and fails by degrees, never by death (§12).
2. ITS ONE OWNER. This volume owns the `paragon` mark and its normalizer (`src/domain/paragon/`), the weekly choice, the gifts' rows and the closed `PARAGON_LEAN_SEAMS`, the retainer, the town's-gate default `gateDefault` for every actor kind, the court's belief of a hidden employer (P3 `paragonBeliefs`), THE CORPS' arm of the mark and THE PATRON DEITY's one lean `swornLean`.
3. WHAT IT READS. BELIEF for everything a paragon or a court decides about another: the settlement's beliefs plus a capped carried picture (§4.7), RN's fame and regard through the rumour road, `readBeliefRelationship`, `believedWhereabouts`, `believedEmployerOf`; TRUTH only in the omniscient arm, for a deciding court's own facts, and for ONE public fact of another court — the crown's proclaimed ban at a member (CH7, §2.2 `gateDefault`, §11 step 2).
4. WHAT IT WRITES, one writer each: the ledger record through `moveNpcRecord` (the ledger's one writer); deeds only through RN's `mintDeed` / `strikeDeed` (§2.2b); P3 through CH's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE; bans as the ledger's own `exclusions` (CH12).
5. WHERE IT MOUNTS. Its pass REGISTERS in RN's one pre-return mount in `envoyPulse.js :: advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse` (THE ONE PERSON STAGE); its birth-side rows in `stressorGates.js :: STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`; its run-side leans at seams that already take `worldState` (`religiousContest.js :: advanceReligionStates`, `pactFormation.js :: reserveFor`, `pestilenceKernel.js :: advanceSettlementPestilence`, `warPeaceDecision.js :: readWarPeaceDecision`); its legs through `npcCirculationTransit.js :: planWanderLeg` → `namedPersonTransit.js :: openNamedPersonLeg`; zero lines in `pulseKernel.js`, `applyWorldPulse.js` and `warTermination.js` (CH17).
6. FLAGS: `paragonsEnabled` · `paragonGiftsEnabled` · `paragonRetainersEnabled` · `paragonGateEnabled` · `paragonCovertEnabled` · `paragonCorpsEnabled` (§19), every one premium. HEARING KINDS: `retainer_offer` · `paragon_resigns` · `champion_duel` · `person_at_the_gate` · `person_banned` · `banished_found` · `paragon_disgraced` · `polarity_turn` · `never_line_tested` (§18); no custody kind. CHAIN: C12 THE CHAMPIONS in the fold's §6, waves CH-0 to CH-11 (§20).
7. DEPENDS ON: RN (fame, regard, the deed ledger), the world-NPC ledger (W-H1/H3), ES (custody ES-2b; the errand-row slot), PL (crown reach, the crown's ban), WC (free units), THE BODIES (BD), ST, PR, PS, OP, MN, HB's habit curve, W-LIVES, W-COIN and the generals design (C4). READ BY: PT (`gateDefault`), RN (the `paragon_act` registration), BD (`isWardenParagon`, `securityOfficeOf`, the one leader record), PR and MN (the lean seams), OP and PS.
8. THE WORDS NOT TO CONFUSE: `champion` the polarity value versus the landed pending-edit op `'champion-npc'`; `swornDeity` the paragon's oath versus `patronDeity`, the SETTLEMENT's seated patron; "the town's gate" (admission) versus "a stressor gate" (`STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`) — "gate" is never written bare (§2.3's map register).

**The dispatch gate is the owner's:** nothing here builds before the UI + prose release is pushed and deployed; push · merge · deploy · migration · dispatch stay the owner's. The rulings in §23 are by delegation under the owner's words ("i want you to rule on the decisions. i give my permission now and for whatever is emergent"; "I defer all judgement and decisions to you and give you permissions needed now and those that will be emergent in the future"), vetoable by one clause.

And on 2026-10-18 — THE OWNER'S DOCTRINES OF 10-18 (BOARD 01:31, 01:33, 05:57, 06:32, verbatim there), applied to this volume: (1) THE WORLD HOSTS THE CAMPAIGN, NEVER COMPETES WITH IT — a paragon has no plot of its own and no agenda for the party; its requests, offers and visits are ignorable at no cost to the story (§16); (2) PURPOSE ONLY BY THE DM — "they have no purpose or destiny unless directed by the user, but if left alone, then absolutely correct" (CH3, §3, §4.1); (3) MACRO OVER MICRO — no trials, no person-scale ledgers beyond what reaches the table (the town's gate, the ban, the hearing); (4) NO ENGINE MORTALITY CLOCK — a paragon's lifespan is the DM's through species and character (CH3); (5) MEDIEVAL FOR MILLENNIA — no era changes a paragon's world; (6) MENACES NEVER EMERGENT — a beast-caller never raises or steers one (§6.2). And the owner's question of 06:35 — "Regarding weather and Sea route isn't this already in the books?" — YES, and CH builds on the books rather than beside them: a paragon's legs are priced by the one modality-aware read `distanceRead.js :: pathCost`, which already carries TRADE M8's sea lanes with their storm seasons and the canon's frozen seasonal overlay (SEASONS-B); CH mints no weather and no sea law (§5).

**THE ANSWER IN ONE PARAGRAPH.** A champion or a villain is a person the DM introduces — never the engine — and gives a calling (one of eight: faith, war, diplomacy, information, health, the wildland, trade, order), a side, one defining trait, a goal, a line they will not cross, and how sure their hand is. They are not a new kind of creature: they live in the world's one ledger of named people, so they travel the roads every named person travels, can be barred from a town, and are spoken of the way everyone is. Each week each one decides, for reasons of their own — their nature, their purse, their moods, their habits, what they have heard and whom they love or hate — whether to stay or go, where, and whether to use their gift where they stand. A saint who preaches tips the faith contest toward her creed; a doctor eases the plague; a magistrate calms the unrest; their villains do the opposite, always through the one truth each system already keeps and always by a bounded amount. Some are bound to a court as its officer and move inside its lands or on its errands; others are free, take work while it suits them and leave when something better comes. Courts hire the ones they have heard of; some hire villains in secret, and pay for it when the secret gets out. Every act that touches another court — an offer of service, a contest between a champion and a villain, a town's gate refused, a ban, a fall from grace — is a hearing the DM sees in its week and may leave, tilt or decide. On the surface the DM reads what happened ("Sister Aude has come to Tolle; the Lantern's faithful grow bolder"); the reasons are there for anyone who looks.

A champion or a villain may also be sworn to a god the DM has made. What the world records of that god — good or evil, lawful or not, warlike or peaceful — leans the paragon's own choices a little and never more than its own nature: a good god's healer stays where the sick are, a lawful god's captain keeps her bargains longer, a warlike god's champion goes to a fight more readily and a peaceable god's less readily. A town that seats the same god lets its sworn one in more easily; a town where that faith has been put out of the temples watches it. The faithful remember what their sworn one does, warmly or bitterly. The god itself never acts.

**Binding prior words.**
- The owner's nine messages above; message 6's rulings: death never the engine's · the number the DM's · courts may hire villains, clandestinely, exposure through the spy system damages the court · no successor ("that is entering into the fate area").
- ODQ §125 item 5 (owner): "an NPC roaming settlement-less may still be captured by bandits and the like, held an APPROPRIATE time, then released — NO ransom is issued (no settlement tie to ransom against)".
- THE DEITY DOCTRINE (ADDENDUM 15): the followers act, the god does not. The carve-out's record (the owner, 2026-09-12, verbatim): "dieties can have prose that act according to their alignment position and related to their rank in the hierarchy of that settlement." and "or it would be correct to say that the followers of the diety acted that way." — the deity's axes (`alignmentAxis`, `lawAxis`), the DERIVED temper (`deityTemper`; the stored `temperamentAxis` INERT) and the pantheon rank (`rankAxis`) are one fact family, the creed's standing IN A TOWN (`cult · established · ascendant`, the one patron, `suppressed`) another: "TWO DIFFERENT RANKS". THE PATRON DEITY (CH22, §10b) reads them so.
- THE PROMISE: a seed is a starting world forever; lived history immutable; tuning owner-signed.
- THE EVIDENCE LAW (10-02/03): who, which power, what effect; complete, not cryptic.
- BEHIND THE SCENES (10-17): immersion behind toggles, complexity only on purpose; UNPREDICTABLE IN PROSPECT, LEGIBLE AND COHERENT ON SCRUTINY.
- Requirement 24 (the hearing week), restated 10-17: "Hearings only happen on the week."
- OD-FC-4: W-OPS is not sanctioned; ES's volumes are the authority. OD-FC-39: `npcConsequencesEnabled` lights in C1's unit (dramatic, full). OD-FC-42: HB stays institution-grain.
- WC §1.12.5 (e): the mercenary FORCE MARKET is parked (an owner question); WC boundary 1: "world-only; no rival adventuring parties". The owner, 2026-10-18 ~07:3x, verbatim: "Mercenaries are fine as is. And effectively can be emulated or used for our champion system just replace a champion with a corp" — the parked force market CLOSES INTO CH as THE CORPS (§16b); `mercSupplementOf` stays untouched.
- The owner, 2026-10-18 ~01:4x, verbatim: "well they have no purpose or destiny unless directed by the user, but if left alone, then absolutely correct." — a paragon's PURPOSE comes only from the DM (CH3).
- THE MAP BUILDER'S HOST REQUIREMENTS (`refs/HOST-REQUIREMENTS-2026-10-18.md`, attached by the owner on 2026-10-18: "consider the attached documents for the architecture as well from our mapbuilder program working in parralel"; ) and the six host rulings of record (BOARD 2026-10-18 00:41): the world canon is the carrier of every physical-world arm (H5's per-seat queries and world points; H22's canon born with every map-module campaign); the WORLD-SITE RECORD (ENC-HL-01..04) is the owner's, slotted, and CH's places gain a `site` arm when it lands; every registry row names its WHERE (ENC-HL-13); FP map pieces are port messages (H17's movers, "projected for the audience"; the map's law 6, "Show presence, not intention"); "gate" is never written bare.

**Population facts that bound the design.** Paragons are FEW by construction (the DM authors each one; the number is the DM's, no engine cap); a world with none is byte-identical to today. Every landed person lane is zero-RNG (labelled FNV / `hash01`), so a paragon's draws never move the pulse stream. The person substrate is dark in every shipped preset today, and its first lighting (OD-FC-39) is dramatic and full only: until a later lighting act, CH is dark in the default preset `realistic_regional` (§19).

---

## §1 THE LAWS (CH1–CH22)

**CH1 — ONLY THE DM INTRODUCES A PARAGON.** The engine never creates, promotes or replaces one. The DM introduces as many as the story wants (the owner: "champions is left to the DM on the number"); there is no engine cap and no successor, apprentice or heir (the owner: "no on a successor because that is entering into the fate area"). The door is a typed DM act (§3): campaign-scoped first (CH-0), a reusable library entry later (CH-0b, a migration that is the OWNER'S HAND).

**CH2 — ONE PERSON, MARKED; NO PARALLEL NPC SYSTEM.** A paragon is a person on the world-NPC ledger (`npcLedger.js`, durable `wnpc_` id) carrying ONE conditional, drop-when-empty field, `paragon`. Identity, whereabouts, transit, bans, the reputation facets RN folds, the DM's verbs and death are the ledger's, unchanged. The mark never sits on a roster record (positional, rebinds on reroll — `generateNPCs`: `` npc.id = `npc_${idx + 1}` ``) or on `npcStates`/`npcGrowth` (positional keys). Every write rides the ledger's one writer (`moveNpcRecord({ patch })`); the field's normalizer is a pure leaf `normalizeRecord` calls, landed IN THE SAME COMMIT as the first writer, because the closed normalizer drops an unknown field on every read (executed, `cure/L-CH.md` M1). The mark has TWO holder kinds, `person` (this law) and `corps` — a free company, the mark on WC's free-unit record (§16b); "no parallel NPC system" holds for both: a corps is WC's unit, a person the ledger's.

**CH3 — DEATH IS NEVER THE ENGINE'S, AND NO FATE IS RESOLVED.** No engine path kills a paragon, picks a `death` close, or decides a paragon's ending; a paragon fails by degrees (CH12's ladder), loses fame and regard, and is never held by the engine — its custody waits on ES-2b's custody wave (§12.4), and until then the DM's own hand holds it ("held at X", §12.4). No engine clock ages it toward death: its lifespan is the DM's through species and character (the owner, 05:57: "in fantasy settings, there are longed lived or immortal species and characters. this allows the DM to decide"). A paragon's PURPOSE comes ONLY from the DM (the owner's words in §0): its authored goal, line and relations at introduction, a revision through `introduce`, or a direct order through the DM's verbs (`assign`); left alone it follows its nature and those authored aims through the weekly choice (§4) and has NO destiny; the engine never originates a purpose, a goal or a destiny for it. Dying is the DM's verb alone: the KILL verb (`killNamedNpc`) and `undoDmVerb`'s inverse of a first introduction are the only callers of `npcLedger.js :: removeNpcRecord` ("the ONLY function in the estate that can drop a soul"), both inside `npcDmVerbs.js`. A dead paragon's deeds stay lived history.

**CH4 — PRESENCE IS A CHANCE; ACTION IS A CHOICE.** No gift is ever always on (the owner: "Just because a beast master walks into a town doesn't mean that they are going to automatically breakdown defences"). Each week the paragon CHOOSES whether to exert its gift where it stands, by its own nature, purse, moods, habits, ties, retainer and what it believes is at stake (§4). Every choice keeps its top three reasons for the dossier.

**CH5 — EVERY GIFT IS ONE BOUNDED LEAN AT ONE LANDED SEAM, AND NEVER A SECOND TRUTH.** A gift multiplies an input the domain's one truth already reads — a `{ mult, reason }` row in `STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES` (clamped to [0.1, 3] by `clampMult`) on the birth side, and one multiplicand where the run-side seam already takes `worldState` (§6). No second plague, danger, crisis, conversion, battle or market law; no new stressor; no coup term (the coup budget is 0.815 of 0.82). Where the domain's seam is dark (war: the generals design; information: the statecraft chain; trade: EC), the gift is recorded with its SLOT and the paragon still travels, chooses and relates.

**CH6 — VILLAINS ARE THE COUNTERFORCE MADE INTO PERSONS (requirement 2).** Every champion's lean has its villain's lean on the SAME seam, scoring off the same evidence, so a saint and a heretic in one town cancel by construction before the hearing. Every gift also meets the domain's own counterforce (the incumbent creed, the town's defences, the plague's own course, the court's posture) and the paragon's own failure setting.

**CH7 — BELIEF, NEVER TRUTH (Law One).** A paragon acts on what it believes: the belief of the settlement it stands in, plus a capped carried picture (§4.7); courts judge a paragon on what they have heard (RN's fame and regard through the rumour road). Under the omniscient info mode every belief read is the truth, and the volume names that arm everywhere (§19).

**CH8 — ONE PHYSICS, ONE MOVER FAMILY.** A paragon's own weekly choice is its mover: a move decision opens its transit leg directly (`npcCirculationTransit.js :: planWanderLeg` → `namedPersonTransit.js :: openNamedPersonLeg` on `namedPersonLegTicks`, written through `moveNpcRecord`), priced by the one modality-aware read (`distanceRead.js :: hopWeeks` → `pathCost`: sea lanes where the canon carries them, the season threaded — §5); the W-H3 residency loop (`advanceResidency`) is NOT called and its banded stay never holds a paragon. COURT BUSINESS ABROAD: no persisted errand row can name a paragon at master (`ENVOY_PURPOSES` is `['sue', 'self_parlay']`, every row needs a peace offer, and envoys are cast from rosters — §0), so (a) the RECRUITER rides the spine as a PRICED JOURNEY, GR-2's idiom (an `ERRAND_CONSUMERS` row that validates its class through `mintErrandSpine` and keeps its state in CH's own record, never an errand row — §7); (b) a bound paragon's COURT ERRAND and the liaison's run wait on THE OWNER'S errand-row slot (the persisted-shape widening ES owes as its "FREE-STANDING spy row" and INTERIOR left "owner-gated by name"), and the degraded arm is every world's arm until it lands: a bound paragon goes abroad only with its court's army or column, or where the DM sends it (`assign` — the DM's direction, §8). When the slot lands the hand-off is already right: a BOUND paragon at its seat is `placed` there with no residency, transit or assignment — exactly the placement `envoyDiplomacy.js :: activeOwnsLeg` claims (`unclaimedPlacementAt`) — and a FREE paragon takes no court errand (it moves itself). The DM's `assign` is the landed `dmAssignment` transit, and while that transit stands the paragon takes no aim and exerts nothing: ONE mover per record (§4.2) — CH's OWN law for the paragon pass, built on the two landed refusals of `npcDmVerbs.js :: assignRoamer` that both keep one mover per record: `active_errand` beside a live envoy errand ("opening a DM assignment beside it would create two lifecycle owners") and `no_route` where the lifecycle is lit and the record already carries a leg or a `dmAssignment` ("An existing leg has no exact settlement from which a new ruling can honestly depart"); it is never a general landed rule. A paragon WITH an army or a column mirrors its leg (a co-location read; the column and army records gain nothing). No second speed. Without a canonized map the paragon hops the relationship graph at `UNPRICED_LEG_WEEKS` (one week a hop).

**CH9 — BOUND AND FREE ARE DISTINCT BUT RELATED (the owner).** A BOUND paragon is an officer of one court, always retained: BOUND = AN OFFICE. At its employer's seat its record is `placed` there (`placed.hostSettlementId` = the seat, no `residency`, no `transit`, no `dmAssignment`) — the office kept where the court sits, and the placement both landed reads already understand: `envoyPulse.js :: envoyIsPhysicallyHome` counts it home ("`String(record.hostSettlementId || '') === originId` && no transit, residency or assignment") and `envoyDiplomacy.js :: activeOwnsLeg` would claim it for an errand (`unclaimedPlacementAt`). When it walks its own leg it is re-filed a roamer in transit (`moveNpcRecord` with `hostSettlementId: null` — the envoy family's own idiom) and re-placed at the seat when it comes home; so `hostSettlementId` stays a PHYSICAL fact, and the employer still lives ONLY in `retainer.employerId` (the seat placement is where the office is kept, never a second employer field). It moves on its own initiative inside the employer's lands (a town, or the polity's members where PL's crown reach is above zero — §5), and outside them only with the court's army or column, where the DM sends it (`assign`), or — when the owner's errand-row slot lands — on the court's errand (CH8) (so a bound magistrate can still answer a call in a member town while errands are unavailable). A FREE paragon is a roamer that goes where its goals lead and takes temporary retainers that last "until a better deal or reason comes along". Leaving a bound office is RESIGNING: it becomes free, it is priced (regard, trust), and it is heard.

**CH10 — NO PERSON HOLDS COIN.** A retainer's TERMS are a band the court sets and the paragon weighs; no coin moves until the treasury's one upkeep law carries a retainer — a NEW `UPKEEP_LEDGERS` row `retainers` in the treasury's own module WHEN W-COIN LIGHTS (the same slot as ST's `sites`; a W-COIN inbox row; its leaf-first car carries the court's retainer roll on the settlement record `treasury.js :: advanceTreasury(settlement, options = {})` already receives — it takes no `worldState`, and its one caller is the frozen kernel; `treasuryEnabled` is lit nowhere today) — so the terms read as paid; the paragon's purse is a DERIVED band, never a coin store (debt stays owner-gated). The court's terms ceiling is the BAND, always on, from ST's one settlement band (§7); the COIN is W-COIN's `retainers` row.

**CH11 — ONE FAME, ONE REGARD, ONE CREDIBILITY (RN).** CH mints no reputation, no regard store and no fame law. A paragon's fame, the regard it holds and is held in, and its credibility are `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_RN.md`'s (the one renown model for every person, `DESIGN_FP_PARTY_BRIEF.md` §5); a paragon's acts are DEEDS on RN's deed ledger with RN's inherent value (party brief §8). CH reads them and supplies the acts.

**CH12 — ONE BAN, ONE TOWN'S GATE.** A ban is the landed `ExclusionEdge` (`banishment_edict`), keyed by an actor ref; the town's gate is ONE default leaf, `gateDefault(actorRef, …)`, owned here with an arm per actor kind (person · party · corps — PT cites it; the corps arm is a FREE company's, §11, CH-10) (THE PASSAGE sends a free corps to this town's gate, PS1 (d), PS §5.5): the ban first, then the crown's ban, circumstance and renown read against belief (`rejectionFor`'s table for persons). A ban is PROCLAIMED by the court's BENCH and the town's gate KEPT by its WATCH (RN's `actingOfficer`; the ruler where the office is empty) — one leaf, one reason vocabulary, one office per act, and "gate" is never written bare. Belief rules both sides: a court bans and refuses on what it has heard; a paragon may not yet know it is banned; a court may not recognise a banished paragon who arrives unannounced.

**CH13 — EVERY ENGAGEMENT WITH A COURT IS HEARD, AND ONLY IN ITS WEEK (requirement 24, extended by OD-CH-3).** An offer of service, a resignation, a contest of champion and villain, a town's gate refused, a ban, a banished paragon found, a fall from grace, a turn of side, a line tested — each is a registered engagement kind (§18). A hearing is minted only at a one-week advance and lives that week; month, season and year advances run every one on its default. Nothing queues. Where the party is touched, the default is always that nothing has happened to the party yet.

**CH14 — ONE LEARNING LAW, ONE DRIFT LAW.** A paragon's habits are the habit curve (`habitCurve.js :: applyCredit`) at person grain, its law word read by `lawWordFor` from its authored alignment's law half; its drift is W-LIVES drift (`characterDrift.js`), funnel-written, on the durable id. Never `npcGrowth`'s trait candidates and never a third carrier (the generals design is amended by a note at its own compile, R1 Q4).

**CH15 — THE FOLLOWERS ACT, THE GOD DOES NOT.** A saint serves a CREED: it reads the creed's recorded axes, its settlement standing and the faith contest's odds as it believes them. Deities hold no authored relations (none exist in code); a god's relations are the derived `stanceOf` plus the CREEDS' emergent regard, written by their followers' history, never a change in a god's mind. `stanceOf` is read only through its two CONSUMED fields (`cooperation`, `betrayalHazard`); `aggression` is an owner-gated build-ahead and is never read; an authored deity-relation field is the owner's slot CH-0c (§3). A paragon of ANY calling may be SWORN to one god (CH22, §10b); this law binds the oath: the sworn one acts by its god's recorded character, the god never acts; a saint's god is its creed.

**CH16 — THE NAME IS QUOTED, NEVER PARSED.** The DM's names and the sixteen display nouns (saint, heretic, captain, warlord, envoy, provocateur, spymaster, deceiver, doctor, plaguebearer, warden, beast-caller, merchant prince, speculator, magistrate, agitator) never enter a rule. Rules read the closed fields `domain × polarity` only. Of the display nouns, the labels of `npcAgency.js :: NPC_ROLE_ARCHETYPES` (the role labels `inferRoleArchetype` reads) carry `captain`, `doctor` and `agitator` but neither `warden` nor `magistrate`; those two are landed ROSTER ROLE keywords — `roleCategory.js` lists `'warden'` under `military` and `'magistrate'` under `government`, and `factionRoles.js` maps `warden: 'watch'` — so on master a roster "warden" is a watch officer. THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE of THE BODIES (`briefs/L-BD.md` 41; PR §5.5: "Where the town's public-security office is held by CH's WARDEN archetype") is, for a paragon, ONE predicate `isWardenParagon(view)` = `domain === 'wildland'` on the champion side of its LIVING polarity (`turned` over `authored`, §13) — the display noun is never read; for a court officer who is not a paragon the archetype is that officer's landed roster role, read by the office's own reader (L-BD 23's government-type read, PL's and BD's), never by CH (§5's trail arm).

**CH17 — THE MOUNT RULE.** Zero lines in `pulseKernel.js` (1581), `applyWorldPulse.js` (907), `warTermination.js` (818). Nothing in `npcAgency.js` (796), `armyTransitKernel.js` (798), `roadsKernel.js` (838, baseline) or `informationStatecraft.js` (781). Every CH module is a leaf under `src/domain/paragon/`; every host CH touches has headroom and is named with its line cost at its wave (§20).

**CH18 — THE PHYSICAL WORLD IS A SEAM.** Until the physical world lands, a place is a settlement, a road between two, or the country around one; a region is a named set of settlements; a polity is PL's walk. Every wave is whole without a map (the graph-hop fallback, CH8). Its CARRIER is the world canon (the first host ruling, §0): legs read the host's frozen record (H5's per-seat queries and world points), so on a map-module world whose canon is born with the campaign (H22) a paragon walks priced legs from week one — CANONIZED ≠ PREMIUM (the COMPATIBLE ruling: the world canon is geography, the premium gate sits on the simulator's lanes; CH is premium in every flag, so a free campaign born canonized simply runs no CH); a direction word ("tired of the north") exists only over canon world points; a place gains a `site` arm through THE SITES' host ledger `worldSites` (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_ST.md`, ST's ONE site read `sitesNear`; §5).

**CH19 — BEHIND THE SCENES.** The surface speaks in deeds and the world's words ("she turned south"; "Captain Vey is in Kell and has not taken up arms"); the reasons, bands, odds and habits wait for a look on purpose (the dossier's paragon page, the Why panel). What builds immersion is shown behind toggles: a paragon's piece on the map and its condition words on that piece — sent to the map module as an H17 PRESENTATION message (a mover "projected for the audience": the DM sees every paragon; a covert hire's villain and every belief row fail closed for any other audience), the host's own map layer being the interim mirror; the piece shows PRESENCE, never intention (the map's law 6: no destination, purpose or ETA on the marker — the destination lives in the Herald and the paragon page). Unpredictable in prospect, legible and coherent on scrutiny: surprise is the goal, never a risk; the risks are illegibility, incoherence and runaways, and the DM's undo and total control are the safety net.

**CH20 — DETERMINISM AND THE FORKS.** Every draw is a labelled keyed hash over a stable key (`wnpc`, tick, purpose), never a pulse PRNG fork (the landed person-lane idiom: "ZERO rng draws (every choice is a labelled FNV-1a hash)"); iteration is codepoint-sorted. Every stochastic choice registers its row in `HABIT_FORK_REGISTRY` in the same commit with its `actionVocabulary` (L10(a)).

**CH21 — EVERY VILLAIN'S HIRE IS CLANDESTINE (the owner, message 6: "courts can hire villains. but such hiring is clandestine").** Every engine-made retainer of a villain is `covert: true` — offered "in secret" on the card, never open, by whichever road it came (a recruiter, a villain seeking work, a bid); a DM-introduced bound villain is covert by default and the DM may say otherwise at introduction (the DM's word is canon). A champion's retainer is never covert.

**CH22 — A SWORN GOD LEANS THE CHOICE; THE GOD NEVER ACTS (the owner: "champions and villains should also have a patron diety option that would also contribute to their decision makings").** A paragon MAY be sworn to ONE god the DM has made — P1's optional `swornDeity`, a committed deity embed (§2.1; the OWNER's persisted shape, OD-CH-51) — and a faith CHAMPION MUST be (its god is its creed, §10b). That god's RECORDED character enters the paragon's ONE roll as ONE bounded, named lean, `swornLean` (§10b), in TWO NAMED HALVES, each monotone in its own coordinates: the ALIGNMENT half — the god's reading of the act by `alignmentAxis` and `lawAxis` (`evil01` / `chaos01`) through RN's reading one, mixed with its own flock's tie through RN's reading two where the act is target-dependent (a saint preaching its own god's creed; a fallen one working against it); and the BOLDNESS half — the ONE temper read `deityTemper` (the stored `temperamentAxis` is inert) against the act's force: a warlike god's sworn finds forceful acts easier and gentle ones harder, a peaceable god's the reverse. The lean sits beside the paragon's chart and its habits and never above them: by construction `act` lies in [0.855, 1.155] and `commit` in [0.90, 1.10] (`SWORN_LEAN_WEIGHT` DRAFT 0.10, the boldness half at half of it; MEASURED over CH's registered faces and the 36-god grid, `act` spans [0.885, 1.120]), inside a habit's landed span (`habitCurve.js :: habitFactor`, `HABIT_SPAN: 0.35`), and S62 pins that the `defining` trait's doubled row outweighs it — the follower acts in its own character first. The embed's other keys — `temperamentAxis`, `rankAxis`, `domain`, `characterAxes`, `boonChannel`, `boonStrength`, `baneChannel`, `baneStrength` — are carried and NEVER read (S67). The pantheon RANK leans nothing in a person's roll (the two ranks are different facts); a god's pantheon FORTUNES reach its sworn one, if ever, only as an EVENT heard through W-LIVES's one drift law (a new rung, W-LIVES's), never as a rank read. The creed's STANDING in a town — its seat, its suppression — moves the paragon's welcome at that town's gate by at most ONE step (§11 step 5b); the faithful's REGARD of their sworn one moves by what it does (RN's). No miracle, no omen, no blessing, no divine act; nothing is predicated of the god ("the followers of the diety acted that way"); the engine never writes a religion state or a pantheon entry, and never a deity embed beyond the DM's door's own copy and the normalizer's fixed-point re-pick of that copy (S67). With no sworn god every read is exactly identity; a god NEUTRAL in alignment, law AND temper leans no choice by its character (only its own flock's tie, reading two, moves an act for or against that flock); a god neutral in alignment and law but with an AUTHORED temper leans only through the boldness half (its alignment half exactly 1).
THE NEVER-READ LIST IS CH'S OWN: "carried and NEVER read" and "never a deity embed beyond the DM's door's own copy" are scoped to CH's OWN reads and writes (the roll, the gate, the page, the door, the normalizer). ONE place takes the oath into WORLD state: §6.2's faith carrier (3a), where `attemptEntry` seeds a town's religion state (`religionState.js`: "`const seed = (existingShare = 0) => ({ deityRef: ref, snapshot: deity, …`") whose snapshot the contest then reads (`religiousContest.js :: deityRankStrength`: "`[deity.rankAxis]`"; `faithField.js`: "`if (str(snapshot.boonChannel) === channel)`"). So (3a) hands `attemptEntry` the WORLD's OWN snapshot of that `_deityRef` wherever any religion state already holds one, and the oath's copy ONLY where none does — the one door by which an oath enters world state, named here once; an oath copy older than the DM's last edit never becomes a second snapshot of a god some town already holds. And (3a)'s saint-oath arm is DARK until the god holds a seat somewhere or the faith layer is active (`religionActive`): a saint's oath to a god seated nowhere is admitted as custom content WITHOUT a contest (§10b).

---

## §2 THE ONTOLOGY

### 2.1 The records

**P1 — THE AUTHORED DEFINITION** (what the DM wrote; CH-0 carries it campaign-scoped inside the living field as `paragon.authored`, a bounded snapshot — the deity idiom `deitySnapshotFrom`; CH-0b adds the library entry and the same snapshot is copied from it at introduction):

| Field | Values | Meaning | Read by |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | the DM's text (also `identityFacets.name` on the ledger record) | quoted, never parsed (CH16) | narration only |
| `domain` | `faith · war · diplomacy · information · health · wildland · trade · order` | the calling: which seam the gift leans on, which calls it weighs | §4, §6, §14 |
| `polarity` | `champion · villain` | which way the gift leans | §6 |
| `creedRef` | a deity ref the campaign knows (a seated patron's or a custom-content deity's snapshot) — the faith calling only; the word is reused in its landed meaning (`DESIGN_FP_FAITH.md`'s `.creedRef`); for a faith CHAMPION it is DERIVED from `swornDeity` — written at the door (`creedRef := deityIdOf(swornDeity)`), re-derived by the normalizer (§10b); a heretic's `creedRef` stays authored, never derived | the creed a saint carries in or a heretic works against; the saint's god IS its creed — one choice at the door, never a second truth (`sworn_creed_conflict` refuses a saint with no oath or a mismatch) | §6, §6.2 (3a), §10, §10b, §11 |
| `renown` | `unknown · settlement · regional · realm` (RN's `FAME_SCOPES`) | the legend that precedes it, minted ONCE at introduction as a `legend_recorded` deed (§2.2b) and decaying like any deed | §3, RN |
| `character.axes` | the W-LIVES chart (the deity's `characterAxes` tokens `CANDOR … CONTENT` × virtue/vice × `a_touch · marked · defining`); EXACTLY ONE position at `defining` | the full personality; the one `defining` position is the bolstered trait | §4 weights; W-LIVES drift |
| `alignment` | `npc/npcFacetContract.js :: NPC_ALIGNMENTS` (the exported eight, `lawful_good` … `chaotic_evil`) — the landed eight carry NO `chaotic_good` (the host document's alignment-16 defect): a chaotic-good champion cannot be authored until that cure lands; the authoring pool offers the eight, and the slot is the fold's alignment owner rows | law half → `lawWordFor` (habits); moral half → the inherent-value reading (RN) | §4.4, RN |
| `ideal` | the ten ideals (order · mercy · prosperity · tradition · security · freedom · knowledge · glory · faith · justice), exported once as `npcFacetContract.js :: NPC_IDEALS` by CH-0's car with a source pin that it equals `npcAgency.js`'s private `IDEALS` (the 796-line host is never touched) | values: what a call or an offer is weighed against | §4, §7 |
| `goal` | `npcFacetContract.js :: NPC_GOALS` (the exported catalog's keys) | the personal goal set against the calling (proposal 3) | §4 |
| `neverLine` | `never_betray_employer · never_serve_rival_creed · never_take_covert_work · never_abandon_a_call · never_harm_the_helpless` | the one line it will not cross, each keyed to an act in its own choice set (§4.2) | §4, §13 |
| `failure` | `fated · sure · fallible · frail` (ordered; index = how far a failure falls) | how often its efforts fail and how far (§12) | §12 |
| `tie` | `bound · free` | CH9 | §7 |
| `employerId` | a settlement id (bound only) | the court it serves | §7 |
| `homeId` | a settlement id or absent | where "go home" leads; homesickness pulls here | §4 |
| `secretKind` | `hidden_employer · past_crime · old_debt · false_name · secret_faith` (the prose stays the DM's, in the NPC `secret` idiom "Known only to the GM") | the one hidden fact the spy system can uncover (proposal 5) | §9 |
| `relations[]` | `{ target: actorRef, band }` — `band` is RN's regard ladder word | authored BASELINES, written at introduction through RN's `DECLARE_STANCE` door as `declared: <band>` (RN's widening of `declared` from `ally · enemy` to a band baseline; `ally`/`enemy` stay its aliases), so emergent terms decay toward them; NEVER a `dmSet` (which never decays and ignores every deed) | §10 |
| `bans[]` | `{ scope: 'settlement' \| 'polity' \| 'region', ids: [settlementId…] }` | authored bans, minted as `indefinite` exclusion edges at introduction (§11) | §11 |
| `holderKind` | `person · corps` (absent ⇒ `person`) | who carries the mark: a ledger person, or a free company on WC's free-unit record (§16b) | §16b |
| `heldAt` | a settlement id the DM wrote, or absent | THE DM'S HAND (custody's only arm until ES-2b, §12.4): while set, the paragon stands there "held", neither moving nor exerting; written and cleared ONLY by the DM's `introduce` revision — authored whereabouts, never an engine hold | §4, §12.4 |
| `heldOffice` | `public_security`, or absent (bound only) | THE DM'S APPOINTMENT of a BOUND paragon to its employer's PUBLIC-SECURITY OFFICE (THE BODIES' item 41: "holding the public-security OFFICE"; the office's ways belong to "the person HOLDING the public-security office with the warden archetype … never to any retained warden"): read only through `securityOfficeOf` (§2.2); ONE holder per court — `introduce` refuses a second appointment to an office already held by a paragon, with a receipt naming the holder, until the DM's revision clears the first; the office lapses with the bound tie (a resignation, the employer's end, a revision to `free`), never by an engine act; absent ⇒ the paragon is an officer of its court in its calling with no named seat (CH9) | §5 trail arm, BD §2.3 · §9.11 |
| `swornDeity` | a COMMITTED DEITY EMBED — `deityCommitEmbed.js :: commitDeityEmbed(ref, snapshot)`'s frozen record `{ _deityRef, name, alignmentAxis, temperamentAxis, rankAxis, lawAxis, domain?, …DEITY_AUTHORED_CHARACTER_KEYS }`, built at the DM's door from a DM-authored deity (its ref `customRegistry.js :: mintDeityRef(raw)`, the ref a town's seat takes) or a god this campaign's world carries (its state key verbatim); or absent (the default) — of its keys the engine reads only `_deityRef` (identity, `deityIdOf`), `alignmentAxis` · `lawAxis` and, through `deityTemper`, `authoredTemper`; the rest are carried, never read (CH22) | the god the paragon is SWORN to (the owner: "a patron diety option") — any calling, either side, a person or a corps; REQUIRED for a faith CHAMPION, whose `creedRef` is then DERIVED from it (one choice at the door, §10b); its recorded character leans the one roll (CH22); "the rest are carried, never read" is scoped to CH's OWN reads — the ONE door by which the oath enters world state is §6.2 (3a): `attemptEntry` takes the world's own snapshot of the `_deityRef` where any religion state holds one, the oath's copy only where none does (CH22) — and the seeded state's `rankAxis`, `boonChannel` and kin are then the CONTEST's reads of a town's religion state, never CH's of the paragon | §10b, §4.3–§4.5, §6.2 faith (3a), §7, §11 step 5b |

`heldOffice` is the DM's appointment because the warden's ways are the OFFICE's, never any retained warden's (THE BODIES' office read, BD §2.3, §9.11 (1)(i): "a paragon by CH's `isWardenParagon` … HOLDING the office"; purpose only by the DM, CH3); the alternative — every BOUND warden paragon its court's office-holder (CH9's "BOUND = AN OFFICE") — would give two bound wardens one office.

**P2 — THE LIVING PARAGON** (`spatialLedgers.npcLedger.{roamers|placed}[wnpcId].paragon`; conditional at every level; absent ⇒ the record serializes exactly as today):

```
paragon: {
  ref: string,                 // the definition's stable ref (CH-0: 'campaign:<fnv>'; CH-0b: the library uid)
  authored: { …P1 },           // the bounded snapshot; edited only by the DM's verbs (§3)
  aim?: { purpose, destId, sinceTick, reasons: [≤3 reason words], exert?: true, bodyId? },   // the standing commitment (§4.3); `exert` = it CHOSE to use its gift this week (§4.5) — the decision, read by no seam; `bodyId` = the quarry of a `hunt_body` aim only (§4.2; THE BODIES' `bodyId`)
  //   aim.roadOpportunity?: { kind, atId, tick, verdict? } — THE OPPORTUNITY's aside mark on a paragon's aim (OP §2.1, §9; its ten paths OP §13 L4; at most one per aim, OP4; `detourFrom` always absent — CH is a paragon's mover): written ONLY by CH's weekly writer at OP's request, kept by `normalizeParagon` from the SAME commit that first writes it (garbage drops the key, never the aim), gone with its aim; the OWNER's persisted shape (OD-OP-24 ⭐ VISIBLE, §23)
  exertion?: { tick, outcome, settlementId },   // the week's exertion, outcome ∈ exerted · faltered · voided (§6.3, §12, §13): EVERY seam reads THIS, never `aim.exert`; `settlementId` = the settlement the exertion SERVED — where it stood, or the town whose riders it rode with (§5 (d)) — so a seam reads it THERE and nowhere else
  //   an exertion that answers a `captive_held` call carries that call's `source.kind` (OP §9's proposal, PLAUSIBLE; the key's name fixed by CH-1's car-0 token census, the fold §5's to register) so the calling stay (§2.2b), `paragonLean` / `paragonRows` (§6) and `patrol_will` SKIP it — the exert at the venue is THE RESCUE CARD ONLY (§4.5)
  calling?: { settlementId, sinceTick, weeks },   // the open stay in its calling — STATE until it closes; its ONE deed is minted at the close (§2.2b)
  soughtBy?: [≤2 { courtId, atId, arrivesAt, termsBand, covert?: true }],   // recruiters on the road toward where each court believes it is (§7; the priced journey)
  banKeys?: [≤8 { settlementId, episode, untilTick }],   // the banning court's seat EPISODE KEY at each emergent ban's mint (PL's `seatEpisodeKeyOf`, §11) and the court's own WINDOW at that door (`untilTick`: the stricter of its emergent windows, as the landed merge keeps it), so the court's sentence survives a later merge into the DM's indefinite edge and the DM's undo of it (§11)
  postId?: string,                // a BOUND paragon's station abroad, recorded when the DM's `assign` brings it outside its lands (§8)
  turned?: { polarity, atTick },   // the LIVING side after a heard turn; `authored.polarity` is never rewritten by the engine (§13)
  retainer?: { employerId, tie, sinceTick, termsBand, covert?: true, exposedAt? },  // §7, §9; `exposedAt` latches the scandal once per hire (§9)
  leads?: { bodyId, sinceTick },  // THE RETAINER KIND `leads` (THE BODIES' item 32): the named gang or conclave this paragon LEADS, set only by the DM's naming act (§7); beside `retainer`, never inside it; THE ONE LEADER RECORD — THE BODIES store no copy and read it through `bodyLeaderOf` (§7)
  habits?: [≤24 { key, stock, lastTick }],      // integer ten-thousandths via habitCurve.roundToUnits (§4.4)
  moods?: { wanderlust?: { stock, awayFrom? }, homesickness?: { stock }, dread?: { stock }, shame?: { stock } },  // §4.5
  picture?: [≤3 { subjectId, bands, atTick, source }],   // the envoy closed-picture idiom (§4.7)
  carried?: [≤4 { subjectId, factKey, atTick, source }], // facts it carries (a liaison's refresh, a deceiver's lure)
  conditions?: [≤3 { kind, untilTick }],        // injured · sickened · overtaxed (§4.7)
  marks?: { metParty?: true, curiosityAt?: tick }  // one-way facts (§16)
}
```

Fame, regard and credibility are RN's (CH11); bans are the ledger's own `exclusions` (CH12); whereabouts are the ledger's `residency` / `transit` / `dmAssignment` / `placed.hostSettlementId`. FILING: a FREE paragon is always a `roamers` record; a BOUND paragon is `placed` at its employer's seat while it stands there (the office, CH9) and a roamer while it walks or rests elsewhere; the DM's `assign` places any paragon where the DM sends it; every own move re-files the record to `roamers` (`moveNpcRecord` with `hostSettlementId: null`), and a bound paragon's arrival at its own seat re-places it there. The employer lives only in `retainer.employerId`. The purse and the needs are DERIVED each week and never stored (§4.7). A turn's one record is `turned` (§13), a crossing's its `line_crossed` deed on RN's ledger, and the arcs read those; the liaison is the home court's officer at that town (§8), never a field on the retainer.

**P3 — THE COURT'S BELIEF OF A HIDDEN EMPLOYER** (CH-7's car): `spatialLedgers.paragonBeliefs[observerId][subjectId] = { employerId, band, atTick }`, `band ∈ vague · uncertain · confident · certain` (the landed belief-confidence words, `BeliefDivergenceBand.jsx :: CONFIDENCE_LABEL`, reused in their landed meaning), at most four rows per observer, drop-when-empty, written by the ONE PERSON STAGE reading this tick's leak-telling ARRIVALS (the rumour stage persists only `rumors.next`, so nothing is written inside it) and by an espionage `confirm` / `refute` product of subject kind `paragon_employer` (§9); the scandal's latch is the retainer's `exposedAt`, never a belief row. It is LIVED BELIEF (persisted), not a derivation. Its persisted rows take a sub-ledger name of their own so the spatial-ledger coverage walker classifies one key; confidence is the landed belief-confidence band, never a float in a receipt; `proven` (a landed literal in `sendTwoDivergence.js`) is not minted; the READ is `believedEmployerOf` (§2.2). The row's SUBJECT is the hidden hand — a paragon's `wnpc_` id, or a BODY's id (`body:<kind>:<fnv>`, THE BODIES §2.2: a gang a court holds `employed`, or a gang or conclave a foreign court SPONSORS) — so the shape reads `paragonBeliefs[observerId][subjectId]`; the two id forms never collide (`wnpc_` · `body:`); the four-row cap per observer counts both; the ONE writer stays CH's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE for every subject (§9's three ties). The names `paragonBeliefs` and `paragon_leak` hold ONE meaning each ("a court's belief of a hidden employer"; "the leak of a covert hire"), whatever the hire's subject: the `paragon` prefix names the idiom's OWNER (CH's module, as `paragonLean` does on MN's and PR's seams), never its subject; the alternative names `hiddenEmployerBeliefs` and `covert_hire_leak` are not minted.

### 2.2 The derived reads (pure, lazy, out of the first-paint closure)

| Read | Signature | What it returns |
|---|---|---|
| `paragonOf` | `(worldState, wnpcId) → ParagonView \| null` | the ONE view reader of the field (with the mark predicate `isParagonRecord` below, the only two; a source-scan census pins no other dotted reader — the generals' `generalOf` precedent) |
| `paragonsOf` | `(worldState) → wnpcId[]` | every marked record, roamers ∪ placed, codepoint-sorted |
| `paragonsAt` | `(worldState, settlementId) → wnpcId[]` | who stands in a settlement this week (residency, placement, an arrived leg) |
| `weeklyChoice` | `({ worldState, snapshot, wnpcId, tick, board }) → { purpose, destId, exert, reasons }` | the week's decision (§4), pure, keyed-hash draws |
| `callBoard` | `(worldState, snapshot, observerId) → { [domain]: Call[] }` | the calls in each domain as the observer KNOWS them — by presence, by a telling's arrival or by a belief band, the truth only in the omniscient arm (§14) — memoized per snapshot |
| `paragonLean` | `(worldState, settlementId, domain, seam) → { mult, reason, wnpcIds } \| null` | the net lean at a seam this week, from each paragon's `exertion.outcome` ONLY (`exerted` applies; `faltered` and `voided` contribute nothing; an `indecisive` duel leaves both `exerted`) — never `aim.exert` (§6). It is ONE bounded MULTIPLIER on the consuming seam's own quantity (an `indecisive` duel's two factors cancel): `seam` is a word of CH's closed `PARAGON_LEAN_SEAMS`, each with its declared deviation `d` — `menace_siege_draw` 0.05 (MN §5.2's keyed verdict draw) · `menace_raid_wear` 0.10 (MN §5.5's wear read) · `patrol_rider_quality` 0.10 (PR §10.5's warden riding with a town's riders, applied as `quality × mult`) · `patrol_will` 0.10 (PR §3.1's WILL gate: a paragon of the `order` calling whose `exertion.outcome` at that town is `exerted` — a magistrate `1 + d`, an agitator `1 − d` — read by PR as `min(1, WILL_SHARE × mult)` in the weeks the exertion stands; never a band step, never "for a season"), DRAFT, UNMEASURED; each paragon whose `exertion.outcome` there is `exerted` contributes `1 + d` (a champion) or `1 − d` (a villain), `mult` is the product, and the consumer clamps it by its own cap and applies it as `x × mult`, NEVER as an additive shift; `mult > 1` reads the champion's way on that seam (MN orients its draw so) On EVERY seam word both sides contribute (CH6: the villain's lean on the champion's seam) — on `patrol_rider_quality` a villain of the wildland calling exerting with a town's riders (a beast-caller among them) is `1 − d` (§5 (d), PR §10.5) — and a paragon counts at `settlementId` only where its `exertion.settlementId` IS that settlement. An exertion that answered a `captive_held` source contributes NOTHING at any seam — no magistrate's ×0.8 row, no agitator's ×1.3, no `patrol_will` (PR's WILL gate reads no lean from it): the exert at the jailer's town is THE RESCUE CARD ONLY (OP §9 "THE EXERT IS THE CARD ONLY"; §4.5). |
| `isParagonRecord` | `(record) → boolean` | the MARK predicate for a landed reader that already holds a ledger record (§2.4): `src/domain/paragon/paragonMark.js`, ZERO imports (so a reader in the first-paint closure pays one tiny module); with `paragonOf` the only two readers of the field (the source-scan census pins no third) |
| `believedWhereabouts` | `(worldState, observerId, wnpcId, tick) → settlementId \| null` | where a court BELIEVES a paragon is: the `actAt` of the newest deed WHOSE DOER is the paragon (`deedIndex(...).byDoer`; a deed done by someone else about the paragon places nobody) by RN's ONE HEARD TEST at the observer's town — `heardAt(worldState, deed, observerSettlementId, tick)`, whose `doerKey` (the doer AS HEARD) must name the paragon, newest `heardTick` first (ties by deed tick, then id), its place the deed's `actAt[0]`, else its first witness (RN §2.3, R55); a version heard under ANOTHER doer places nobody — fame, regard and the whereabouts read one heard doer (RN OD-RN-31); in the omniscient arm the newest of its deeds inside whose projected reach the town lies, AT ONCE (`hopDelayTicks` reads 0 there — RN §2.3, the landed identity) — the recruiter's destination (§7) |
| `gateDefault` | `({ worldState, snapshot, graph, actorRef, settlementId, tick }) → { verdict, reason, officer, believed }` | the town's-gate default for EVERY actor kind (person · party · corps), one reason vocabulary (§11); PT's `party_gate` calls it. A SHARED LEAF THAT DECIDES: it reads BELIEF wherever it reads another court's STATE — the pair label through `readBeliefRelationship`, the served court as the deciding court believes it, renown through RN's heard test — and TRUTH for the deciding court's own facts (its own edicts at its own door, its own wars) and for ONE public fact of another court, the CROWN'S PROCLAIMED BAN at a member (§11 step 2): an edict the crown proclaims to its members is public at their doors as a declared relationship is (`readBeliefRelationship`'s own reason: "the declared relationship is public, so absence is NON-paranoid"), so step 2 reads the proclamation as truth and only the HOLD it is weighed by through the member's belief; CH, its owner, names this ONCE, here, and no caller re-reads (the shared-leaf rule: "a shared leaf reads BELIEF when it DECIDES for an actor … its owner names which, once; a caller never re-reads") |
| `believedEmployerOf` | `(worldState, observerId, subjectId) → { employerId, band } \| null` | P3, read; `subjectId` a paragon's `wnpc_` id or a body's id (§9's three ties) |
| `isWardenParagon` | `(view) → boolean` | THE WARDEN ARCHETYPE for a paragon (THE BODIES' item 41; PR §5.5): `domain === 'wildland'` on the champion side of its LIVING polarity; the one predicate PR, BD and §5's trail arm read — never the display noun (CH16) |
| `securityOfficeOf` | `(worldState, courtId) → wnpcId \| null` | the paragon HOLDING `courtId`'s public-security office: the one paragon whose `authored.heldOffice` is `public_security`, whose living tie is `bound` and whose `retainer.employerId` is `courtId` (P1); `null` otherwise — then the office is the roster's (THE BODIES' read, BD §9.11 (1)). THE BODIES' office read (BD §2.3 `knownTrailsOf`'s office arm, §9.11's leader (1)(i)) calls it and applies `isWardenParagon` to what it returns: an office held by a non-warden paragon carries no trade (a noble without the trade knows only what the town has learned); memoized per snapshot |
| `purseBand` / `needsOf` | `(worldState, wnpcId, tick) → band` / `→ { safety, coin, purpose, renown, belonging }` | derived bands (§4.7); `needsOf`'s ONE reader is §4.4's score, each need an URGENCY MULTIPLIER on its one matching term (§4.7) |
| `swornDeityOf` | `(view) → DeityEmbed \| null` | the paragon's sworn god (P1 `swornDeity`) or null — the one reader of the field, inside `paragonOf`'s view (no third dotted reader); a COURT reads it only as a PRESENCE fact at its own town's gate, and never under `secretKind: secret_faith` (§10b); a far hearer knows the oath only as a PART of the telling it heard (RN's) |
| `swornLean` | `(view, { purpose, quarryKind, targetCreedRef }) → { act, align, bold, commit, reason }` (`purpose` the candidate's — the exert decision passes `work_here`; `quarryKind` only for `hunt_body`; `targetCreedRef` the creed the act helps or hurts on a target-dependent face — the saint's own, a heretic's target, a hunted conclave's — else absent) | THE ONE SWORN LEAN (§10b), in two named halves over CH's own registered face of the act (§2.2b): `align = 1 + SWORN_LEAN_WEIGHT × m`, `m` RN's reading of the act with the god as holder — reading one with the god's force coordinate held at ½ (its alignment terms only) over `(a + b) / 2`, mixed by RN's reading two at μ ½ where the face is target-dependent (the god's own creed helped +1, hurt −1, any other creed no tie); `bold = 1 + ½·SWORN_LEAN_WEIGHT × sgn(force01 − ½) × T`, `T` = +1 · 0 · −1 for `deityTemper` `warlike` · `neutral` · `peacelike`; `act = align × bold`; `commit = 1 + SWORN_LEAN_WEIGHT × (2·lawfulness01 − 1)`. All exactly 1 with no sworn god or an act-less purpose; `align` exactly 1 for a god neutral in alignment and law on a face with no tie; `bold` exactly 1 for a neutral temper or a face at force ½; DRAFT, UNMEASURED (FP-46) |
| `swornTemperOf` | `(view) → 'warlike' \| 'neutral' \| 'peacelike' \| null` | the sworn god's temper WORD for the paragon page's scrutiny line — the same `deityTemper` read `swornLean` makes, exported by the same leaf so the page never calls `deityTemper` itself and the leaf stays the ONE new caller the landed census counts (CH-11's car-0) |

### 2.2b The acts CH hands RN (the deed source `paragon_act`, RN's `DEED_SOURCES`)

RN owns the deed ledger (`spatialLedgers.deeds`, one writer `src/domain/renown/deedLedger.js :: mintDeed / strikeDeed`, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_RN.md`) and its source row `paragon_act` is CH's to fill: the paragon stage mints, through RN's writer only, one deed per act of this closed kind list — `paragon_exerted` (ONE deed per STAY IN ITS CALLING, MINTED WHEN THE STAY CLOSES: the stay opens as STATE at its first `exerted` week in a town — `calling: { settlementId, sinceTick, weeks }`, `weeks` counting the `exerted` weeks — and nothing is written to RN's ledger until it CLOSES, at departure, after a season (whichever comes first), or when the DM's `kill` removes the record (the kill verb's paragon arm closes the stay before `removeNpcRecord`, and that kill's undo strikes the deed by RN's `strikeDeed`); at the close ONE deed is minted through `mintDeed` with its magnitude from `weeks` and its target the town, append-only and never amended — a stay with no `exerted` week mints nothing; never a deed a week) — a dark-after-lit freeze keeps the open `calling` and resumes it, and a fame read during the stay sees the stay's earlier deeds only: the open stay is a fact of the paragon page, not yet of the world's tellings · `legend_recorded` (the DM's authored `renown` at introduction: doer the paragon, witness the introduction town, its significance set so RN's own `projectScope` reaches the authored scope where the world's carriers allow; struck with the introduction's undo; it decays like any deed) · `paragon_faltered` (a stumble) · `paragon_disgraced` · `retainer_broken` · `office_resigned` · `duel_won` (the doer the winner, the target the loser) · `line_crossed` · `side_turned` · `covert_hire_exposed` (the doer the EMPLOYER court, the subject the paragon) · `barred_at_gate` (the doer the court, the target the paragon) · `sold_out` (a corps' sell-out, §16b) · `hunt_won` — THE ONE DEED OF A LED HUNT'S SUCCESS (the ruling of record: "the hearing's OWNER mints: a LED hunt's success is ONE deed minted by the hunt's own door — CH's `hunt_won` at the mission's close, doer the LEADER (`ledBy`), target the body"): minted ONCE when THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` mission closes with the body `snuffed_out` or `driven_off` (the card's words, item 40), by CH's door `paragonHunt.js :: mintHuntWon` reading the card's CLOSING ROW in THE ONE PERSON STAGE (the closing row is SP-9's record; BD names it), through RN's writer only; the DOER is the leader the card names as `ledBy` — a paragon (`p:<wnpcId>`), a corps (`u:<unitKey>`), or a NON-PARAGON public-security office-holder on the person ledger (`p:<wnpcId>`; BD §9.11's leader (1)) — CH's door mints for every leader the card can name, so no led hunt has a second door; the target the body by its RN key (`g:<bodyId>` for a gang, the creed's `creed:<deityRef>` for a conclave, and for a beast pack — which holds no key — the posting court as the deed's place and witness); its `sourceEventRef` the `hunt_taken` engagement's id, so RN's cross-door idempotence refuses any second mint of the same success; a claimed BOUNTY is this same row (BD §9.9's claim — "no third deed"; item 33: "a claimed bounty is a DEED (RN) with the body's defeat as its outcome"), its doer the claimer; THE PATROLS' `patrol_clash` mints NO deed for a led hunt (its row is the clash's record; an UNLED clash's deed is PR's own, the TOWN its doer — PR's and RN's halves); a hunt that ends `escaped` is the BODY's deed (RN's `escaped` row), never a CH deed; a FAILED hunt the leader led is CH's `paragon_disgraced` for a paragon (§18) · `warden_attempted` (a body's attempt on a WARDEN — a bribe or a strike — heard as an arm of §11's road arm; the doer the body by its RN key — a NAMED gang's `g:<bodyId>`, a nameless body minting nothing — the target the warden; ONE deed per card at its close, whatever the verdict). The person's key on RN's ledger is RN's `p:<wnpc>`; a corps' is RN's UNIT key `u:<unitKey>` — never `c:`, the court's prefix — and it is the doer of its `sold_out` deed (RN's `doer` gains `corps`, RN's row). THE INHERENT VALUE of each act is REGISTERED by CH in the table below and DERIVED by RN from that table only — RN derives nothing CH did not register, and both volumes say this sentence (the table is CH-3's registration row in RN's §6.1). Every deed's `causedBy` is the paragon's receipt for that week (requirement 20). CH reads back `fameOf` / `fameIn` (the gift's scale, the town's-gate belief, the recruiter's choice, the call board's "a renowned healer is called") and `regardOf` (proximity, offers, bans), and nothing else of RN's. The kind words are compounds, census-checked at CH-1's car-0.
THE CALLING STAY'S CARVE-OUT: an exert that answers a `captive_held` source (the HELP call at the venue, or the villain's `harm` twin there, §14) neither OPENS nor ADVANCES `calling`, so no `paragon_exerted` is ever minted for it at the jailer's town; its ONE deed is OP's `opportunity_act` (`captive_rescued` on `freed`, `rescue_foiled` on `caught`; nothing on `turned_back` or `refused`), its `target` the JAILER `jailerOf(hold)` — the occupier in an occupied town, never the occupied court (OP §5.3, §9) 

NO HEROIC-FACED ACT IS A FALSE REPORT: any paragon — champion or villain, of any calling — may take OP's `false_word` (OP §7.1) through the one roll (the owner: "Envoys, champions, spies, etc could also provide false information"); it is registered here as the paragon arm of the ONE kind `false_word_exposed`, on the DECEIVER's (villain-side) face (the `false_word` row of the table below), minted at the exposure only, shameful — a champion that lies does a villain's deed, the DM's to mark (§13's turning point); of any sworn paragon the lean says "less readily", never "never" (§10b)

ONE DEED PER ACT, MINTED AT EXPOSURE ONLY: a paragon who SPEAKS a false word mints NO `paragon_act` deed — a lie unexposed is no deed (belief never truth: nothing is known until IN's reconciliation or a witness exposes it); the act's ONE deed is the ONE kind `false_word_exposed` (RN §3.1's `opportunity_act` slot row; OP §7.1's EXPOSURE), minted AT THE EXPOSURE, whatever the speaker; CH REGISTERS the paragon arm of that one kind — the FACE the one roll reads (the deceiver's; §10b's sworn lean weighs it inside `weeklyChoice`) and the doer — never a second deed. The sworn lean's executed figures were measured on the `information` villain row's coordinates (0.30 · 0.25 · 0.50); on this row's set they are UNMEASURED — PLAUSIBLE that every stated order holds, since `kind01` and `law01` stay below ½ and the lean is monotone per half (S68); re-measured at CH-11's car; the coordinates are DRAFT, UNMEASURED and vetoable | the DECEIVER's face (villain side, whatever the speaker's polarity) | 0.25 | 0.3 | 0.5 | no (false reports) | shameful — ONE set of DRAFT coordinates, OP's (OP §2.4's proposal; RN §6.1's `false_word_exposed` row), RN signs at the FP-46 roster |

Every cell is DRAFT and UNMEASURED, on the FP-46 roster (OD-CH-27); the rows are RN §6.1's THREE COORDINATES (`kind01`, `law01`, `force01`, 0.5 = no signal) so RN's `paragon_act` block and its registration walker take them as they stand, the face band following from RN's `kind01` edges; the DM overrides any deed's weight by RN's door. CH SUPPLIES its values as a registration row at CH-3 through `inherentValue.js`'s registration door.

### 2.4 The landed readers of the ledger's two maps — every one declares its paragon arm

A DM-introduced saint carries an empty `originRef` and would otherwise be told as a displaced person on the DM's own surfaces, drafted into a roster vacancy or counted against the roaming ceiling. Census at master `81f1cd963`: every module that reads `npcLedgerOf(…).roamers` / `.placed` (15 readers; `AutoplacementConsent.jsx` and the two demographics modules match only an unrelated `.placed`). Each reader's arm is keyed on one predicate, `paragonOf(worldState, wnpcId) !== null`; CH-0 lands a CENSUS PIN over this list — a new reader reds until it declares its arm.

The census (`rg -l "\.roamers|\.placed\b" src`) returns 18 files; less `AutoplacementConsent.jsx` and the two demographics modules, FIFTEEN real readers, each declared below, and every EXCLUDED reader's guard is costed in effective lines (comments and blanks excluded, the house's count — `envoyPulse.js` reads 332 by it, as §0 has) against CH17. Every guard reads the ZERO-IMPORT mark predicate `paragonMark.js :: isParagonRecord(record)` (§2.2), never a dotted `.paragon` read.

| Reader (`path :: symbol`) | What it does with every record | The paragon's arm | Host lines (eff. now → ≤ after) |
|---|---|---|---|
| `components/map/heraldWanderers.js` (THE HERALD's Wanderers register) | lists every roamer and placed person; `CAUSE_LINE.none` "They keep their own counsel about why they left." | EXCLUDED — a paragon speaks on its own page and in its Herald beats | 166 → ≤ 169 (the import; one filter on each of the two `pool` reads) |
| the settlement dossier's unaffiliates section (the local projection of the same ledger, `npcLedgerProjection.js`) | lists residents with no faction | EXCLUDED there; the town's dossier carries a "paragons here" line instead | (with the next row) |
| `npcLedgerProjection.js` (audience projection; "Roamer records carry DM TRUTH") | strips covert truth for non-DM audiences | INCLUDED with a paragon arm: the DM sees all; any other audience sees the name, the display noun and where it stands — never a covert retainer, a covert hire's employer, `secretKind`, `soughtBy`, `heldAt`'s author note or a belief row; the non-DM allow-list also carries the PUBLIC OATH ("sworn to <god>") beside the display noun — never under `secretKind: secret_faith` (then DM-only, §10b; L1c veil) | 113 → ≤ 119 (the import; the unaffiliates exclusion; the audience arm; the oath a one-entry widening, no new line) |

It meets a paragon only once the owner's errand-row slot lands (CH8) | 332 → 332 (zero lines) |

## §3 THE DM'S DOOR — INTRODUCTION, REVISION, AND THE DM'S OTHER HANDS (CH-0; CH-0b)

**The act.** `introduce` — a fourth `DM_VERBS` value (a declared design amendment: "A fourth verb is a design amendment, not a call site"), driven from the paragon page in Edit Mode (premium, behind U16) — THE PARAGON PAGE is a CONDITIONAL HERALD DOOR on the landed Wanderers door's own idiom (§20 CH-0 names the host and its line cost). The DM fills P1 from closed pools (the name is free text) and says where the paragon is: a settlement where it rests (free) or its employer (bound). The verb:
1. mints the durable id with `mintDurableNpcId` over a stable key built from the definition's ref (CH-0: `campaign:<fnv(name, tick, ordinal)>`; CH-0b: the library uid), zero-RNG and idempotent — the `graduateNpc` properties without a roster;
2. writes the record — a FREE paragon into `roamers` with a `residency` at the chosen settlement; a BOUND paragon `placed` at its employer's seat (the office, CH9), its employer written into `retainer.employerId` — with an EMPTY `originRef` — a DM-introduced person has no roster origin, so no regeneration fold ever touches it (`regenIdentityFold.js`: the ledger half "filters on `originRef.settlementId`");
3. writes `paragon.authored` (the bounded snapshot) and nothing else of the living field;
4. seeds the authored relations as RN `declared` baselines through RN's `DECLARE_STANCE` door, the authored bans as `indefinite` exclusion edges through `addExclusionEdge` (§11), and the authored `renown` as ONE `legend_recorded` deed through RN's `mintDeed` (§2.2b);
5. emits one authorship receipt and one dossier line ("Sister Aude, a saint of the Lantern, is in Tolle").

Re-issuing `introduce` with the same ref REVISES the authored snapshot (the living state — habits, moods, retainer, aim — is untouched; a changed `tie` or `employerId` re-places the record through `moveNpcRecord`). Its inverse is an arm of `npcDmVerbs.js :: undoDmVerb` — the removal call stays INSIDE `npcDmVerbs.js`, and `paragonIntroduce.js` builds the record and the undo token and never names `removeNpcRecord`: it restores the prior snapshot, or, for a first introduction, removes the record through `removeNpcRecord` ("the ONLY function in the estate that can drop a soul") and strikes the `legend_recorded` deed it minted. The law-1 pin `tests/domain/npcDmVerbs.test.js` ("ANCHORED NEGATIVE: no engine lane can reach the removal door; only the DM verbs do", scanning `['src/domain/worldPulse', 'src/store']`) widens its scan to `src/domain/paragon` in CH-0's commit, its allowed set unchanged (`npcDmVerbs.js`, `npcLedger.js`).

**The DM's other hands, all landed or one amendment each:** `assign` (send it somewhere — the landed verb and its transit; for a bound paragon sent outside its lands, the DM's direction that makes it an operative abroad, §8), `kill` (death — the DM's alone; for a corps, disbandment, §16b), `pardon` (lift a ban), `banish` (author a ban — CH-5's amendment, §11), and HOLD — the `introduce` revision writing `authored.heldAt` (the paragon stands "held at X", neither moving nor exerting, until the DM's revision clears it; the DM brings it there first with `assign`), custody's only arm until ES-2b (§12.4); a hearing's three choices on every engagement (§18); RN's regard door for any relation; the paragon's piece on the map (a toggle). Every engine-written paragon state is either reachable by these or recorded "engine-only, because X" in §21. These doors are the ONLY sources of a paragon's purpose (CH3) — what it was authored to want, a revision, a place it was sent; the engine supplies consequence, never an aim of its own.

**CH-0b — THE LIBRARY BUCKET (the OWNER'S HAND).** Reuse across campaigns at deity parity costs (R1 §2 Q6, CONFIRMED by enumeration): a manifest category `paragons` with `authorableBuckets` and `lanes[living].buckets` rows and per-field `effect`/`activation`/`consumers` (a field with no named consumer is presentation-only); the regenerated `customContentManifest.generated.js` and its edge twin (`scripts/generate-custom-content-manifest.mjs`); ⛔ a Supabase migration widening `custom_content_category_check` ("drop + re-add; the only safe way", the 049/155 precedent) and every archive/restore function in `188_reviewed_supply_chain_persistence.sql` that enumerates buckets literally; the promotion contract's `authorableBuckets` and an observation probe per mechanical field; the authoring UI (`components/compendium/CustomContent.jsx`), sample fixtures and usage echo; and the walkers that govern those directories. The migration is owner-gated: CH-0b is recorded with its slot after CH-0 and builds only on the owner's word (OD-CH-2). The world copies the library entry into `paragon.authored` at introduction (the deity's snapshot idiom), so a library edit never silently rewrites a living paragon.
A LIBRARY PARAGON'S OATH: a library entry's `swornDeity` is a CROSS-BUCKET DEITY REFERENCE the `paragons` manifest row must carry (the deity bucket's ref, `mintDeityRef`), copied by `commitDeityEmbed` into `paragon.authored` at introduction (§10b; L1c create) — the OWNER's hand with CH-0b (OD-CH-2)

**CH-0c — THE AUTHORED DEITY RELATION (the OWNER'S HAND; slot beside CH-0b).** The owner's message 7 asks that deities too, "when they are formed and this can also be authors in custom content", carry relations. Shape: a manifest field on the deity category (`relations[]` of `{ target: actorRef, band }` in RN's ladder words), copied into the deity snapshot at seating (the `deitySnapshotFrom` idiom), and an owner-deployed migration on the legacy `custom_content` CHECK — the `supabase/migrations/200_deity_authored_character.sql` pattern ("Nothing in the application waits on this: the JS admission wall … already refuse[s] a malformed value"). Read ONLY through the followers (the deity doctrine): an authored relation seeds the CREED body's `declared` baseline in RN (`creed:<deityRef>`), so the faithful act on it and the god does not; `stanceOf` stays the derived core. Builds only on the owner's word (OD-CH-12).

**EDITOR (L10's five members).** (a) forks: none (the act is the DM's); (b) seals: no process — `introduce` is a DM verb with its own receipt; (c) counterparty: `real-only` — a paragon is introduced only at a real settlement, never a phantom; (d) states: the stage casts no roster person and so never reads `isOffStage` for a paragon; a paragon's death is `killNamedNpc`; (e) record: worldState-side lived history inside the already-classified `npcLedger` sub-key; nothing on the settlement record.
