## §4 THE WEEKLY CHOICE — PRESENCE IS A CHANCE, ACTION IS A CHOICE (CH-1)

**4.1 Where and when.** One leaf pass, `src/domain/paragon/paragonStage.js :: advanceParagons`, REGISTERED in RN's ONE PERSON STAGE (fold §3.1.22): RN owns the single pre-return mount in `envoyPulse.js :: advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse`, ABOVE its `envoyDiplomacyActive` early return, and runs in a closed, pinned order RN's heard pass → CH's pass → OP's stage → PT's engagement pass; CH adds NO host line of its own. The pass is gated by `paragonsEnabled === true` ∧ `npcConsequencesActive(worldState)`. CH REQUIRES of RN's mount (its car-0): the pass's `worldState`, `wizardNews` AND `settlementUpdates` thread into BOTH the early-return object and the full path — the host's full path reads the original parameter (`let updates = settlementUpdates;`) and the early return returns its input, so CH's court hits (§9) would be lost unless that binding threads too — and RN's stage MEETS it: it rebinds the three parameters `worldState`, `wizardNews` and `settlementUpdates` from its result before ENC-3's drop pass and the early return (RN §5.1, ≈ 4 host lines), and with the envoy gate shut the function hands back the very `worldState`, `wizardNews` and `settlementUpdates` it was given (EXECUTED, `cure/D-RN-r2b-probe/earlyReturn.out`), so CH's court hits survive both returns; the harbouring write (§11) needs only `worldState` and the graph the stage already receives. It runs after rumours and beliefs have moved this tick (the kernel's own order: "Rumors and beliefs move first so the traveler's closed picture can hear only what is available where they stand") and once per weekly kernel call (an interval advance is N weekly calls, so a month runs it four times on defaults). Paragons are visited in codepoint order of `wnpcId`. A stage below the early return would run only where all six `ENVOY_REQUIRED_RULES` are lit (measured, `cure/L-CH.md` M2), so the stage mounts above it.

**4.2 The closed choice set.** Each week the paragon holds one AIM: a PURPOSE and a destination settlement id; staying is the aim whose destination is where it stands (derived, not stored).

| Purpose | Destination | Allowed when | Mover |
|---|---|---|---|
| `work_here` | where it stands; for a `captive_held` source the call STANDS AT its `source.venueId` — the exert gate's "here" (§4.5) | a call in its domain stands here, or it is retained here, or — a bound paragon posted abroad — this is its post (`postId`, §8); for a `captive_held` source, the paragon has arrived at the venue | none (it stays); EXERT is decided this week (4.5); at a `captive_held` venue its exert is THE RESCUE CARD ONLY (OP §9; §4.5) |
| `seek_work` | a court it believes needs its calling and fits its values or pay | free, or bound and resigning (§7) | its own leg |
| `answer_call` | a place whose call in its domain it believes is the gravest within reach; for a `captive_held` source the destination is `source.venueId` (the `hunt_body` precedent), never the call's settlement | free; bound only inside the employer's lands — and for a `captive_held` source a BOUND paragon answers it only when the call's settlement (the captive's HOME) is its employer or its employer's ally (`ALLY_LIKE`) and the jailer (OP `jailerOf`) sits at a T4 rung toward its employer, never where the jailer is its employer or sits at no T4 rung toward it (the friend rule, OP10: a jailer allied to the employer forbids it; an `allied_hall`'s jailer is the hall's lord); a FREE paragon answers by its own roll | its own leg (bound: inside the lands; beyond them only by its employer's `court_errand`, never offered until the owner's errand-row slot lands — and that errand to a `captive_held` venue rides the landed purpose class `covert` (master `envoyErrandVocabulary.js :: ENVOY_PURPOSE_CLASSES` = `commercial · covert · diplomatic · factional · personal · religious` — no `order`, so §5's "classes … by domain" names none for the `order` domain): a court sending its champion to break a captive out of another court's cells is a covert act of THAT court, ES's leak law applying, while the CALL's domain stays `order`, named once in THE OPPORTUNITY §9 (OD-OP-33)); a FREE paragon's rescue writes REGARD ONLY, no court-level favor (OP §6, §9) |
| `keep_rendezvous` | the meeting place: the member of its employer's lands nearest its post | bound, posted abroad by the DM's `assign` (`postId`, §8) | its own leg, out to the meeting and back to its post |
| `take_rest` | where it stands, or the nearest place it loves | a condition is active, or `overtaxed` | none / its own leg |
| `return_home` | `homeId` (bound: the employer's seat, where it is re-placed on arrival, CH9) | it has a home | its own leg |
| `pursue_nemesis` | where it believes its nemesis is | an RN regard link at `enmity` or worse with a paragon of its domain and opposite side, and a nature that seeks (4.4) | its own leg |
| `hunt_body` | the quarry's PREDICTED place by the hunting court's ONE prediction, `predictedPlaceOf(worldState, snapshot, <the hunting court>, aim.bodyId, tick)` (PR §2.6, on that court's belief — never the body's true place), walked to that place's settlement (a stretch's or a trail's end on the hunt's side, the country's settlement, a site's attached settlement); `null` ⇒ it waits where it stands, as the riders do | it is NAMED the hunt's leader on THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` card (item 40's preference: first the court's public-security OFFICE-HOLDER — a paragon holds it only by the DM's appointment, `securityOfficeOf` (§2.2) — then the card's CHAMPION ARM names a paragon RETAINED by the court, or the DM names one; a free paragon becomes retained by answering the court's bounty, §7); the quarry a NAMED body (item 32b) the hunting court pursues — `hunted`, `outlawed` or a beast (BD §7.1; PR §5.5's quarry rule: a `curbed` or `permitted` conclave never); by default a champion of the `wildland`, `war` or `order` calling, any paragon by the DM's word | its own leg — never PR's mover (PR §5.5: "PR NEVER MOVES A PERSON'S RECORD"); a BOUND paragon beyond its lands only where the court's detachment rides (PR §5.5's pursuit), never alone; it may take the ways it KNOWS (§5's trail arm: `knownTrailsOf`, and a warden HOLDING the office its office's ways) |
| `wander` | a neighbour it has not lately visited | free, nothing stronger | its own leg |
| `visit_party` | where it believes the party stands (PT's `whereaboutsOf`, as heard) | §16 | its own leg |
| `court_errand` | where its employer sends it | bound; the court's business — ONLY WHEN THE OWNER'S ERRAND-ROW SLOT LANDS (no row can name a paragon at master); never offered until then | the errand family, claiming it at its seat (`unclaimedPlacementAt`, CH9) |

"Its own leg" means the pass opens the transit leg directly (CH8). `court_errand` is never a candidate until the owner's errand-row slot lands; the rendezvous is walked on the paragon's own leg from a post the DM chose (§8); the recruiter is a priced journey (§7) and needs no row. A HELD paragon (`authored.heldAt`, §12.4) takes no aim at all: it stays, and exerts nothing.

**One mover per record; the DM's post holds.** (1) THE DM'S ASSIGNMENT IN TRANSIT. Where the lifecycle is lit (`routeNetworkLedger.js :: routeLifecycleActive`, `routeLifecycleEnabled` false in every preset today — so this arm is latent), `npcDmVerbs.js :: assignRoamer` writes a `transit` leg and a `dmAssignment` (`{ targetSettlementId, atSettlementId, issuedTick }`) and `advanceAssignedNpcTransits` walks it after the person stage, placing the record at the target on arrival (`placeNpcRecord(state, wnpcId, target, …, { residency: null, transit: null, dmAssignment: null })`). A paragon whose record carries a LIVE `dmAssignment` TAKES NO AIM AND EXERTS NOTHING until that placement: the pass skips it before the weekly choice, opens no leg of its own, writes no `aim`, no `exertion` and no `calling` week (an open calling stay closes at the assignment's departure and mints its one deed, §2.2b), and the assignment's arrival is the DM's placement, never a town's-gate case (`assignRoamer` already reads the shut doors and asks the DM to override, `excluded_without_override`). Mid-leg the DM cannot assign it where the lifecycle is lit (`npcDmVerbAuthority.js :: assignmentOrigin` reads no origin for a record in transit, so `assignRoamer` refuses `no_route`); where it is dark the assignment places it at once (`dmAssignment: null`), its own leg discarded — the DM's word — and it chooses next week from where the DM put it. Two movers never share one record: `assignRoamer`'s own refusal is the law. (2) THE POST HOLDS. A bound paragon placed by an assignment outside its lands is POSTED (`postId`, §8), and a posted paragon HOLDS ITS POST: its own initiative reaches only `work_here` at the post (the row above admits it), `take_rest` there, `keep_rendezvous` out to the meeting place and back, and `seek_work` AS A RESIGNATION ONLY (the `paragon_resigns` hearing, §7 — the one way a posted paragon leaves on its own); `return_home`, `answer_call`, `hunt_body`, `wander` and `visit_party` are not candidates while the post stands, so nothing walks it home between rendezvous — going home is the DM's `assign` home (which clears `postId`). (3) THE LEADER RIDES WITH ITS BODY. A paragon that LEADS a body (`leads`, §7; THE BODIES' item 32) takes no aim of its own while it leads — only the week's EXERT decision where the body stands (4.5) — and it leaves its body only by the DM's act (THE BODIES' `BODY_LEADER` remove, or the naming act's inverse), the DM's `kill`, a heard `polarity_turn` or the body's end (§7): its leg MIRRORS the body's — CH's pass is the paragon's OWN mover and the mirror is what it writes, the body's place its destination each week (BD says the same: "The paragon's leg MIRRORS the body's … the body's place is the leader's destination each week") (the co-location read CH8 gives a paragon WITH an army or a column; the body's own stage moves the body, never CH), and its exertion serves the settlement the body stands at or beside (`exertion.settlementId`); one mover per record holds — the body's stage never writes the paragon's record and CH's pass never moves the body. (Without striking the walking purposes a homesick operative's `return_home` could still outscore `work_here` at the post, which S6 forbids; the alternative — every purpose kept, so a posted paragon drifts home on its own nature — is not taken.) Pins: S6 (it stays between rendezvous) and S43 (an assignment in transit).

**4.3 Commitment and the clock.** The standing aim holds until a candidate's score beats it by `COMMIT_MARGIN` (DRAFT, UNMEASURED — the owner's "until a better deal or reason comes along"); arriving completes a move-aim and the next week's choice starts from the new place. Cadence: weekly. Dwell: none of the W-H3 stay band — a stay lasts until a weekly decision chooses otherwise (`aim.sinceTick` and `COMMIT_MARGIN` carry the commitment, so a paragon neither twitches nor lodges for months) (the W-H3 stay, `RESIDENCY_STAY_TICKS: { min: 4, max: 104 }`, would freeze "tired of the north" for up to two years). ARRIVAL ORDER: an arriving leg is read by the town's gate (§11) BEFORE any lodging write — a refused paragon never lodges inside. Tier: the person tier (§19). A SWORN paragon's `COMMIT_MARGIN` is multiplied by `swornLean(…).commit` (its god's law half, within [0.90, 1.10] — ±`SWORN_LEAN_WEIGHT`, DRAFT 0.10): a lawful god's sworn holds an aim and a bargain longer, a chaotic god's yields to a better one sooner — never a forced break (§10b).

**4.4 The weights (every term bounded, named, and one of the three reasons it can give).** score(candidate) = calling (the call board's band for this domain and side at the destination, as believed, discounted by reach — `reachBandOf(hopWeeks)` or graph hops) + duty (retained: the employer's calls weighted by the paragon's FIDELITY position) + purse (`penniless` / `scraping` raise `seek_work`) + moods (4.5) + relations (RN regard of actors near the destination, direction and strength set by personality, §10) + habits (`habitFactor` on the candidate's habit key, 4.6) + season (the campaigning season draws the war calling; the reach discount reads `hopWeeks(digest, from, to, season)`, so a winter pass or a storm-season sea lane — the canon's frozen seasonal overlay and TRADE M8's sea lanes, both already in the one price `pathCost` — makes a far candidate farther; no CH weather) + goal (its personal `goal` against the calling — a captain whose goal is `settle_rivalry` pulls toward the rival's town even when no call stands there) + site (ST's ONE site read `sitesNear(worldState, settlementId, digest?)`: a candidate with a site that fits its calling weighs one bounded row by `SITE_FIT` — faith a `shrine`, health a `hospice`, war a `fortification`, wildland a `watchtower` or `crossing`, trade a `waystation`, `resource` or `harbour_works`, order a `watchtower`; a villain an UNHELD `fortification` or a `ruin` (a lair in prose); a `tower` by no calling — a held tower is the DM's placement, ST3; DRAFT, UNMEASURED) + the watch (only where THE PATROLS are lit — §5's patrols line: a prudent or cowardly paragon on a covert or hostile errand avoids a road PR's ONE `believedWatch` read says is watched; a warden of the champion side weighs riding with a town's riders — a week in that town, never a mirrored leg, §5's patrols line (d)). Each term is then weighted by its need's URGENCY (§4.7, `needsOf` — its one reader is this score). Personality maps onto these terms through ONE table over the W-LIVES chart (the owner's words, each read from the chart, never from the display noun):

| The owner's word | Chart position (DRAFT mapping, OD-CH-9) | What it does to the choice |
|---|---|---|
| charitable | GENEROSITY virtue | exerts unpaid where a call stands |
| mercenary | GENEROSITY vice | exerts only under a retainer; weighs pay over calling |
| capricious | FIDELITY vice, read at its expression `mercurial` (a DECLARED reading: no axis homes "capricious") | a wider keyed-hash spread on every term; commitments break sooner |
| cowardly | COURAGE vice | avoids destinations with a believed danger (menace, war, a nemesis) |
| brave / defiant | COURAGE virtue | confronts a nemesis; may enter a banned town in secret (§11) |
| patient | TEMPER virtue (`patient`) | waits where it stands for a graver call before exerting |
| prudent | PRUDENCE virtue | takes the long road around enmity |
| vengeful | FORBEARANCE vice (`vengeful`) | seeks out what it hates (`pursue_nemesis`) |
| curious | TRUST virtue (`trusting` — a DECLARED reading: no axis homes "curious"); the party draw also reads HUMILITY vice (`proud`, §16) | the curiosity draw toward the famous (§16) |
| loyal | FIDELITY virtue | duty outweighs nature; resigns rarely |

The `defining` position doubles its row's weight (the bolstered trait). The chart is W-LIVES's CANDIDATE register ("THIS IS A CANDIDATE REGISTER", owner-unsigned), so this mapping is a DRAFT ruling row on the FP-46 roster; it reads the chart only, so the authored personality and the drift law stay one. Every row reads its own axis word (`paradigmAxisCatalog.js :: PARADIGM_AXES` — TEMPER patient/wrathful, FORBEARANCE forgiving/vengeful, FIDELITY loyal/treacherous with `mercurial`); P1 has no flaw field. THE PATRON (§10b): each candidate's score is also multiplied by `swornLean(…).act` — its sworn god's two halves on the act the candidate would make, its alignment reading and its boldness (exactly 1 for an act-less purpose or an unsworn paragon; for a god neutral in alignment, law and temper, moved only by its own flock's tie), within [0.855, 1.155] — inside a habit's span, and S62 pins that this doubled row outweighs it; the pantheon rank is never read

**4.5 The exert decision and the moods.** If the week's aim is `work_here` (or it arrives where its domain calls), it EXERTS when a keyed hash `hash01('paragon.exert.<wnpcId>.<tick>')` falls under p(exert): base by calling and believed stakes (the call's SEVERITY word), raised by duty (bound: FIDELITY), by charity, by a habit of exerting here; lowered by mercenary nature without a retainer, by `dread`, by an active condition (which forbids it), by a cowardly nature where danger is believed. The decision is persisted as `aim.exert` (declared in P2) and its outcome as `exertion.outcome` (`exerted · faltered · voided` — §12, §13); each seam reads the outcome at its own latency (§6.3). Moods are banded stocks on `bandedStock.decayTowardNeutral` (no new decay law), half-life `a_season`, each with its cause: `wanderlust` (rises with the weeks spent in one country; `awayFrom` names what it is tired of: ON A CANON a direction word from a new pure leaf `paragon/bearingWord.js :: bearingWord(fromPoint, toPoint)` over the canon's per-seat world points (H5) — "tired of the north"; ASPATIAL the DM's region name when the country lies in one, else "these lands" — no cardinal-direction reader exists in `src/domain`, and none can exist without world points), `homesickness` (rises with the weeks away from `homeId`), `dread` (after a failure that fell to `disgrace` or a lost duel), `shame` (after a disgrace, a broken retainer, a crossed line). Moods enter the score as bounded terms and as reasons ("tired of the north", "homesick"). p(exert) is also multiplied by `swornLean(…).act` for the exertion's own face (§10b); and `shame` gains one cause — an `exerted` week its own sworn god's ALIGNMENT reading condemns (`m ≤ −½`, its own flock's tie included, §10b — reached by the fallen saint under a good or a neutral-lawful creed, and under a WHOLLY NEUTRAL god (neutral in alignment, law and temper), EXECUTED, `lean2.out.txt`), one step on the same banded stock.
THE RESCUE EXERT IS THE CARD ONLY: for a `captive_held` source "where its domain calls" is the source's `venueId`; arrived there, the exert's ONLY effect is the offer of OP's `rescue_attempt` card and that card's verdict (OP §5.2–§5.3): (i) NO CALLING STAY — §2.2b's `calling` is neither opened nor advanced, so no `paragon_exerted` is minted at the jailer's town; (ii) NO SEAM LEAN — `paragonLean` and §6.1's `paragonRows` find no row from it (no magistrate's ×0.8, no agitator's ×1.3) and PR's WILL gate no `patrol_will`; (iii) ONE DEED — OP's `opportunity_act`, targeted at `jailerOf` (the occupier in an occupied town). HOW: the persisted `exertion` carries its call's `source.kind`, and the stay's opener, `paragonLean`, `paragonRows` and `patrol_will` skip `captive_held` (OP §9's proposal, PLAUSIBLE; P2's comment); p(exert) for it reads the face §10b names (`captive_rescued`), so the sworn lean weighs the rescue itself

**4.6 Habits (one learning law).** A paragon's habits are rows `{ key, stock, lastTick }` (≤ `HABIT_ROWS_PER_ACTOR_CAP` 24) credited by `habitCurve.js :: applyCredit({ stock, lawWord, outcomeRung, direction, ageWeeks, entrySeat: null, currentSeat: null })` — `lawWord` = `lawWordFor(lawfulness01)` of its authored alignment's law half (lawful 1 · neutral 0.5 · chaotic 0, through the landed `LAW_WORD_EDGES`), seats null ("An unresolvable seat identity applies NO shock"). Keys: `winter_at:<sid>`, `serve:<employerId>`, `exert_at:<sid>`, `road:<a>|<b>`. Credit on outcomes (a deed done, a failure, a season rested well); the factor `habitFactor(stock)` multiplies the candidate's score (absent ⇒ exactly 1). Readable on scrutiny: "has made a habit of wintering in Tolle". The word-mapping is the landed reader `lawWordFor`, so a paragon and a court learn by one mapping (`cure/L-CH.md` M9).

**4.7 Drift, the picture, the material life.**
- DRIFT is W-LIVES drift only (`characterDrift.js`, keyed by the durable id; written by the lived-experience funnel, "ONE WRITER"); a paragon's deeds deposit through the funnel. Dark until `characterDriftEnabled` lights (lit nowhere today): until then a paragon's chart is its authored chart, and the choice is still whole.
- THE PICTURE (RF-6): what it believes = the belief of the settlement it stands in (`beliefRecord(worldState, hostId, subjectId)`), stale by the weeks since it stood somewhere that knew better, plus `carried` facts (≤ 4) — a liaison's refresh, a deceiver's lure (a carried fact whose `source` is the liar: they can be deceived, and the liar is traceable) — and `picture` (≤ 3 banded subjects on the envoy closed-picture fields `storesBand · strengthBand · moraleExhaustionBand · foundingCauseStatus · believedRatioBand`). Under the omniscient info mode the picture is the truth.
- NEEDS are derived bands each week, never stored (a stored need would be a second decay law): safety (conditions, believed danger), coin (`purseBand`), purpose (weeks since it last exerted in its calling), renown (RN fame band against its own past peak), belonging (RN regard links at `fondness` or better). THEIR ONE READER is §4.4's score: each need is an URGENCY in [0, 1] (machinery, never shown) that multiplies its ONE matching term by `1 + NEED_URGENCY_WEIGHT × urgency` (DRAFT 0.5, UNMEASURED, FP-46 roster) — safety → the danger-avoidance reading of the personality rows (cowardly, prudent) and `take_rest`; coin → the purse term; purpose → the calling term; renown → the calling term on a candidate whose call stands `grave` or worse (where a deed would be heard); belonging → the relations term toward a place or actor it loves — so a need shifts in urgency (proposal 2) with no stock of its own.
- THE PURSE is DERIVED: `penniless · scraping · provided · moneyed` from the retainer's `termsBand` and the weeks unretained; no coin is held (CH10).
- CONDITIONS (vulnerability without mortality): `injured` (a failure in war or the wildland), `sickened` (exerting where the plague is grave, for a doctor), `overtaxed` (four consecutive weeks exerting, DRAFT); each `{ kind, untilTick }` with a DRAFT banded length; an active condition forbids exerting and raises `take_rest`.

**4.8 The receipt and the felt sentence.** Every choice keeps its top three reason words (`called_by_plague · retainer_duty · purse_empty · tired_of_region · homesick · habit_here · near_nemesis · loves_this_place · fears_this_road · heard_of_party · line_held · keeps_the_faith`), stored on `aim.reasons` — `keeps_the_faith` is §10b's word: the lean was among the three largest terms that raised the chosen aim; the page says "as her faith bids", never "her god wills". SURFACE: a Herald beat only when a paragon arrives at or leaves a settlement where it has a call, a tie or a relation, ranked under the push budget — "Sister Aude has left Kell, tired of the north, and taken the south road." (direction words only over canon world points; aspatially "tired of these lands", 4.5) SCRUTINY: the paragon page lists the aim, its three reasons in words, its habits ("has made a habit of wintering in Tolle"), its moods, its conditions, its relations with reasons. MACHINERY: the weights and draws are never shown. The paragon's piece on the map is a toggle; its condition words ride the piece as toggles (the owner's caravan rule).
`purse_empty` stays CH's reason word for a PARAGON's own purse; THE WAY OF WAR's court-grain deviation cause is `coffers_bare` (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_WS.md` §2.5, `WAY_DEVIATION_CAUSES`: "`purse_empty` NOT used (CH §4.8's reason word for a paragon's purse)") — two words, two subjects, never merged (the fold's §5)

**4.9 Counterforce and clock.** Commitment is broken by wanderlust and by better candidates; habits fade by the curve's half-life; moods decay toward neutral; duty is countered by nature. Requirement 9: cadence weekly; dwell none beyond the weekly decision (4.3); moods `a_season`; habits per the law word's half-life band; drift per W-LIVES. FIRST-VISIBLE: the week it moves or exerts (the Herald beat or the dossier line).

---

## §5 THE ROAD — HOW A PARAGON MOVES (CH-1)

- **Its own leg (free, and bound inside its lands):** a move decision opens the leg directly — `planWanderLeg({ digest, fromId, destId, tick, hiddenHopsOf, season })` → `openNamedPersonLeg` on `namedPersonLegTicks`, written through `moveNpcRecord` (residency cleared, `transit` set; a bound paragon leaving its seat is re-filed a roamer, CH9) — and the pass walks it hop by hop; an arrival is read by the town's gate first (§11) and only then writes the new `residency`; hidden paths at `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` for a defiant paragon entering a banned town in secret (§11). The W-H3 loop (`advanceResidency`) stays callerless: no CH wave mounts it, and ordinary roamers' movement is no CH business (the fold's §2 row and RN §5.4's premise say so). Its `MOVEMENT_SITES` row in `namedPersonTransitTotality.walker.test.js` is the paragon pass module.
- **The trail arm — THE WAYS, ON THE ONE LADDER (THE BODIES §5.2; L-BD 41 · 11c–11i; `hiddenWaysEnabled`).** A WAY is an edge of master's ONE route ladder at its hidden rung (`routeNetworkLedger.js :: ROUTE_GRADES` "`['highway', 'road', 'track', 'hidden']`"; the map contract's class {`wild`, `way`}, worn by use to a `trail`) — never a second network: THE BODIES' ONE read `hiddenWayEdges(worldState, digest)` (BD §2.5) returns each way with its RUNG from the route ledger where the route lifecycle is lit, and THE BODIES' candidate corridors at the same price where it is dark (BD §5.2's degraded arm; master's own `routeNetworkConsumers.js :: hiddenHopsFor` is "NULL on a dark world"). A paragon is a named person on H3's wander physics — the landed `wanderer` kind of `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` — so CH adds NO access token (`mayUseHiddenPaths('wanderer')` holds; THE BODIES add only `body`); a CORPS is a unit and never takes a way by CH's arm ("armies may not", widened only by the declared change THE BODIES name for a wartime unit at WC's and PS's unit, L-BD 11i). WHICH WAYS A PARAGON KNOWS — ONE READ: `knownTrailsOf(worldState, snapshot, <the settlement it stands in>, tick)` (BD §2.3: the town's stored learned set ∪ its public-security OFFICE's derived ways while that office is held by a person of the warden archetype); its picture is that town's belief (CH7), so every paragon knows the ways the town it stands in knows, the office's included where that town's office is so held; and a paragon that HOLDS its employer's public-security office (`securityOfficeOf`, §2.2 — the DM's appointment, P1 `heldOffice`) and is of the warden archetype (`isWardenParagon`) knows that office's ways WHEREVER it stands — the office arm of `knownTrailsOf(…, <its employer's seat>, …)`, within the office's jurisdiction (the home's land; a crown's office the kingdom's by PL's reach, THE BANNER) — because the office's knowledge is the PERSON's who holds it (the owner's word of THE BODIES, verbatim: "i would say that a warden, or whoever you deem is the most appropriate champion archetype, has access to hidden routes network as part of their job"; the ruling of the same minute, L-BD 41: the warden archetype "holding the public-security OFFICE knows every hidden way within the office's JURISDICTION"): it knows them the week the DM appoints it and loses them the week the office lapses (the bound tie ends, or the DM's revision clears it). A warden merely RETAINED — free under a bounty, or bound with no seat — knows only what the town it stands in knows: its job is never its retainer. ONE SEAM, ONE PRICE: CH's road leaf builds ONE callback `hiddenHopsOf(fromId)` — the far ends of the ways `hiddenWayEdges` gives at `fromId` that the paragon KNOWS by the read above — and hands it to master's `planWanderLeg({ digest, fromId, destId, tick, hiddenHopsOf, season })` → `nextHopToward`, whose own rule takes a way only when it lands STRICTLY nearer the destination (a BYPASS `trail:<a|c>` skirting a town's gate, toll and riders, BD §5.2 (ii)), the leg `openNamedPersonLeg`'s with `gradeMultiplier: NPC_CONSEQUENCES_TUNING.HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` (2, pinned equal to the ladder's `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` 2) and `hidden: true`. A way BESIDE the road on the SAME pair (BD §5.2 (i)), which `nextHopToward` never prefers ("Ties keep the road"), is taken only by the paragon's own WEEK when it weighs cover over speed — a hostile or covert errand PR's `believedWatch` says is watched on the road (§4.4's watch term), a defiant entry into a banned town (§11), a hunt it leads on the ways (§4.2 `hunt_body`), a body it leads that keeps to them (§4.2 (3)) — and opens THE SAME LEG, `openNamedPersonLeg({ fromId: a, toId: b, nominalWeeks: hopWeeks(digest, a, b, season), gradeMultiplier: NPC_CONSEQUENCES_TUNING.HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN, hidden: true })` — `planWanderLeg`'s own call with the hop the week chose: one price, one leg physics, one network. There is no second trail price and no `TRAIL_WEEKS_MULT`: the LANDED constant wins, and BD cites the landed symbol. The season rides both networks (`hopWeeks(…, season)`; slow-not-sever). MET by THE PATROLS only on the way's own terms — `trailWatchOf(worldState, snapshot, holderId, trailKey, tick)` (PR §2.6: ZERO on a way the riders' town does not know; it reads the town's known set through `knownTrailsOf`, THE BODIES' route (b) to PR), never the road half's coverage (a paragon on a way is the encounter law's traveller as a body is — PR's); a card raised on a way names WHERE `road (a, b)`, the way named on the card (§18; the fold's WHERE row: "THE TRAIL IS NO SIXTH WHERE KIND"). DARK (`hiddenWaysEnabled` off, or no canon): no way; the secret entry of §11 is the road itself, at the watch's draw.
- **Bound:** destinations are restricted to its employer's LANDS — the employer itself, or, when the employer is a polity's root, the members where PL's crown reach is above zero (PL's ONE reach read — `crownReachAt(worldState, snapshot, rootId, memberId, observerId) → { share01, liegeId, tier }`, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_PL.md` §2: every direct member has `share01 > 0`, and a sub-vassal has it only under a hold firmer than `frayed`); outside them only where the DM sends it (`assign`: the DM's direction; a bound paragon arriving outside its lands records that place as its `postId`, §8), on `court_errand` once the owner's errand-row slot lands (classes `religious · diplomatic · covert · commercial · factional` by domain; the errand family then the one mover, claiming it `placed` at its seat — CH8, CH9), or WITH its employer's army or column (a co-location read: the paragon's leg mirrors the army's or column's — the generals' "a `command` roads mission PINNED to the army" idiom without the mission; the army and column records gain nothing; RF-21), or LEADING its court's hunt where the court's detachment rides (§4.2 `hunt_body`: its own leg on the court's one prediction, PR §5.5 — PR's pursuit beyond the gate is PR's and THE PASSAGE's — PR §10.1 (c)). The DM's `assign` sends any paragon anywhere (the landed `dmAssignment` transit).
- **Aspatial:** without a canonized map (`nextHopToward` returns null without a digest), the paragon hops the relationship graph's neighbours, each hop `namedPersonLegTicks({ nominalWeeks: UNPRICED_LEG_WEEKS })` = one week (RF-18) — an adapter on the one physics, never a second speed. On a map-module world whose canon is born with the campaign (H22) this arm is never taken.
- **With a column or an army:** "the saint joins a group of immigrants, also shifting that immigrant group's configuration of religion" needs WY-4's unbuilt `faithCargo` (`migrationCargoEnabled`) — SLOT C7 (the saint still travels beside the column as a co-location read); "the captain travels with a military unit, or is a general" rides the generals design's `deployment.general` — SLOT C4.
- **No errand row (every world's arm until the owner's slot):** no persisted errand row can name a paragon at master (§0: `ENVOY_PURPOSES` is `['sue', 'self_parlay']`; `normalizeErrand` needs a peace offer; envoys are cast from rosters), so lighting OD-FC-39's unit changes nothing for a paragon. What still travels: the paragon's own leg (inside its lands; between a DM-chosen post and its lands' edge, §8); the RECRUITER as a priced journey with no row (§7). What waits on THE OWNER'S errand-row slot — the persisted-shape widening ES owes as its "FREE-STANDING spy row" (`espionageMissions.js`, R-ES1-1) and INTERIOR left "owner-gated by name" (`emigreErrand.js`, Q-TC12-1): the court's errand abroad and the liaison's run; their named first consumer is the paragon.
- **The map:** a paragon's piece is an H17 PRESENTATION message to the map module (a mover through WY's single projection, "projected for the audience": DM-only for a covert hire's villain; PRESENCE only — no destination, purpose or ETA on the marker), the host-map leaf layer its interim mirror until H19 re-points the host's map screens and H20 retires the fork; the interim layer is ONE ROW in the owned leaf unit FP MAP LAYERS — EP's, cut before the first FP map piece (the fold's §3.3.16 owned-unit row) — whose one import into `WorldMap.jsx` is the car (599 effective at a 600 ceiling by eslint's own count at `81f1cd963`, the host MN, PT and PL also target); CH writes no `WorldMap.jsx` line and builds no car of its own. A place gains a `site` arm through THE SITES (below).
- **The sea and the seasons — already in the books.** The owner's question, verbatim: "Regarding weather and Sea route isn't this already in the books? Because I swear I talked about it like this already." — yes: CH rides the books without a line of weather or sea of its own. A paragon's leg is priced by `distanceRead.js :: hopWeeks(digest, fromId, toId, season)` → `pathCost`, the ONE modality-aware read: TRADE M8's SEA LANES embark at a port ("a sea lane embarks at a port") with their storm seasons slowing, never severing, and the canon's frozen SEASONAL OVERLAY (SEASONS-B) lengthens a winter pass — it SLOWS the pass and never cuts it (`spatialCost.js :: SLOW_NOT_SEVER_MAX = 6`: "NEVER infinite/cut — so a snowed-in town is dear to reach yet still RESCUABLE by spring"; every `SEASON_TERRAIN_COST` value finite and ≤ that cap); the route search `nextHopToward` → `candidateRoutes` walks the same digest, so a saint takes ship where the canon has a lane — and sails past a port without landing there (a coast voyage is ONE leg, port to port, §11: a harbour passed at sea is no arrival — OD-PS-28's one meaning). ONE CURE, CH-1's car: `planWanderLeg` calls `hopWeeks(digest, text(fromId), hop.toId)` with NO season today — it gains a `season` argument passed through (≤ 2 lines in `npcCirculationTransit.js`, 75 effective; the function has no production caller, so the change is byte-identical everywhere), and the paragon pass passes the stage's own `season` (`advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse({ … season })`). The per-settlement CLIMATE BAND is ALREADY landed and read by the food year (`spatialDigest.js :: CLIMATE_BAND_VERSION = 1`; `seasons.js :: climateBandFor`, "W-CAP CAP-3 — this settlement's frozen climate band"); what is owed is the host document's accepted H11 RE-VERSION of it (ENC-HL-09), and travel reads it only through ONE re-versioned `SEASON_TERRAIN_COST` table inside the same price — the band SCALES the slowing, never a second multiplier, never a closure, never a CH term. A paragon riding WITH an army mirrors the army's leg by land or sea; at sea the army is carried by W-NAVY's CONVOY (`armyTransit.js :: ARMY_ROLES.CONVOY` on the sibling `navalTransit` ledger; `navalLayer.js`: "A navy convoys an own/allied army over water"; lit by `simulationRules.js :: WAVES` `navalEnabled: true`), and a paragon riding with a lost convoy's army debarks with it (`navalKernel.js`: the embarked army "SHARES the convoy's fate … a forced debark") — never harmed beyond its leg (CH3). CH mints no sea law and no sea fight. The navy is landed, lit and owner-commissioned (`docs/DESIGN_NAVY.md` on master); CH rests on the landed carriers alone.
- **Sites considered** (THE SITES' coverage row for CH, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_ST.md` §7: "BY THE SITE READ — the call board's `wildland` row; a paragon's choice weighs a post, a tower, a lair; a villain paragon holder is untied"): CH mints nothing for sites and reads them through ST's ONE site read `sitesNear`: (a) the weekly choice's site term (§4.4, `SITE_FIT`); (b) the call board's `wildland` den row (§14); (c) a paragon may HOLD a site — ST's holder class `person`, set by the DM's `SITE_HOLDER` or by taking (ST3: the engine never places a site) — and its sign follows the paragon's LIVING side (`turned` over `authored.polarity`, §13): a champion's site is tied (it benefits the attached towns), a villain's untied (the general hazard), so a heard turn flips the site's sign with no ST change; (d) a person holder's DERIVED funding band is CH's purse band read onto ST's `FUNDING_BANDS` (`penniless` → `unfunded` · `scraping` → `meagre` · `provided` → `funded` · `moneyed` → `lavish`; DRAFT, UNMEASURED), and ST's EFFECTIVE band is `min(fundingSet, derived)` — the DM's band a CEILING (ST6); `meagre` until CH lights; (e) a held site's holder `kill`ed by the DM leaves the site to ST's endings (`abandoned` the next tick, ST S6); (f) every CH hearing's WHERE gains `site` from `worldSites` (§18). Hosts: none of CH's — the rows register in CH's own leaves at CH-1 and CH-2.
- **Patrols considered**: patrols stay a MAP-ONLY picture until the host's PR lights (the host document's newest version: "patrols stay a map-only picture for now"); then the host's patrol facts go OUT THROUGH THE WINDOW like every other host fact (PR14, PR §13: the events window H9 and the week's-presence row H23, and H16 — the map's INBOUND door — stays untouched; CH mints nothing for patrols. When `patrolsEnabled` lights, CH's commit registers four rows and no law: (a) CH's leg choice reads PR's ONE `believedWatch(worldState, snapshot, observerId, roadKey, tick) → { watch01, known }` (its return named by PR) over PR's ONE STRETCH KEY (`road{a, b}`, unordered, keyed `stretch:<a|b>` — the digest's own `pairKey` form; a power's half is the part on its own land, split at the crossing point between the two territories, PR §2.2's `cut01`, 0.5 until the digest carries that fraction) (OD-PR-16: one planning read, never a second); (b) a hostile paragon NOTICED on its leg by PR's one encounter law (`patrolEncounter`, PR8; a paragon's fame cuts its cover through RN's `fameIn` AT THE RIDERS' TOWN — that town's belief, never `fameOf`'s world-wide truth, PR §8.1) is TURNED BACK in CH's own words — a `person_at_the_gate` card whose WHERE is the road (`road{a, b}`, the half on the riders' town's land; ENC-HL-13), default `refused` — and never seized (custody waits on ES-2b); the half is chosen by the LEG's own position — `namedPersonTransit.js :: namedPersonPathPosition`'s leg-wide `progress01` against `(hopIndex + cut01) / last` (PR §2.2) — and a sea hop has no half and no watch (PR §2.2: "water carries no watch"); a paragon of the diplomacy calling is a PERSON on its own leg, never THE PASSAGE's envoy arm (no errand row names a paragon; `envoy_intercepted` / `envoy_held` stay the army interception's); a FREE corps noticed on a road is turned back by the same words (the corps arm, §11), a RETAINED corps is THE PASSAGE's (PS's witness and ladder); (c) a `patrol_report` that has ARRIVED is road (b) of the call board (§14); (d) PR §10.5's warden who rides with a town's riders and the magistrate's order on a town's will register as rows of CH's shared seam leaf — the will through the seam word `patrol_will` (§2.2: an `order` paragon's exertion at that town, `WILL_SHARE × mult` in the weeks it stands) — each receipt naming the paragon. "Riding with the watch" is a WEEK, never a mirrored leg: a patrol is a weekly-placed detachment of the town's own capacity, never a stepped unit (PR1), so the warden's row reads a `wildland` champion whose `exertion.outcome` that week is `exerted` and whose `exertion.settlementId` is the town whose riders walk their round — a week with the watch, or a week it stands with the town's detachment on a hunt it leads (§4.2 `hunt_body`; PR §5.5: "its lean on the riders … applies only in a week it stands with them") — and a VILLAIN of the same calling exerting with them (a beast-caller among the riders) is the same seam's counterforce, a factor `1 − d` (CH6; PR §10.5), and its lift — the riders' quality × `paragonLean(…, 'wildland', 'patrol_rider_quality').mult`, a bounded MULTIPLIER, never a band step (PR §10.5) — reaches that town's own halves (PR's `roundOf`). The lift rides INSIDE PR's leans and inherits their bounds: it lowers only what raiding menaces and untied dens added to the roads (PR5 (a)'s own words) — never the walls term and never a hostile army's or column's presence — and only inside PR's feeder of the ONE capped suppression `min(0.25, σ_site + σ_patrol)` that the SHARED presence composer applies, so the lift can never raise the cap (the composer is a shared car, its builder the first of C14 · C15 · C16 · C10 to land) (PR5 (a); OD-PS-19: "forts and riders never suppress a hostile ARMY's presence"). The liaison's run (§8) is the paragon's own leg, so it meets riders exactly as any paragon does.
- **Bodies considered**: CH mints nothing for bodies and meets them as (a) a COUNTERPARTY on the road or a trail — §11's road arm, `person_at_the_gate` with the body as its party (BD §9.5: "A body meeting a PARAGON on its leg is CH's road arm"); (b) a QUARRY — the purpose `hunt_body` (§4.2), led beside THE PATROLS' detachment (PR §5.5), its win the act `hunt_won` (§2.2b); (c) a body a paragon LEADS — the retainer kind `leads` (§7); (d) a BOUNTY on the call board (§14) answered by a retainer offer (§7); (e) a TIE of the one clandestine-hire idiom — a court's `employed` gang, a foreign court's sponsored gang or conclave (§9); (f) the TRAILS — the trail arm above, on the ONE ladder; (g) a NAMED gang's ATTEMPT on a warden — the road arm's second card (§11). A body NEVER seizes a paragon (custody waits on ES-2b, §12.4; L-BD 34: "a NAMED person taken by a body … is CUSTODY, which WAITS ON ES-2b"), never kills one (CH3), and robs it of nothing (no person holds coin, CH10). Lights with THE BODIES' chain C17 (`bodiesEnabled`; the trails `hiddenWaysEnabled`).
- **Movement considered**: THE MOVEMENT GRAMMAR (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_MG.md`) NAMES a paragon's movement verb from CH's weekly choice (`personVerbOf` — `keep_course` · `reroute` · `hold`, MG §4.2 N-6) for the map's verb word and the Why panel only; MG ROLLS NOTHING for a paragon and CH's roll is unchanged; a paragon on its own aim is CH's mover, and an errand row only on `court_errand` once the owner's errand-row slot lands (OP's `detourEnvoyErrand` then its one in-flight re-plan); the paragon RIDES as CH's aim decides (MG's `RIDING_MISSIONS`: "a paragon by CH's aim"), MG's derived `ridingOf` applying to it as to any person, its gain and access WY's to rule.

## §6 THE GIFTS — EIGHT DOMAINS × TWO SIDES, ONE BOUNDED LEAN AT ONE SEAM EACH (CH-3)

**6.1 The birth side — one row per domain in `STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`.** A leaf `src/domain/paragon/paragonGateRows.js :: paragonRows(snapshot, pressure, stressorType)` returns ZERO OR ONE `{ mult, reason }` per paragon whose week's `exertion.outcome` is `exerted` and whose `exertion.settlementId` IS `pressure.settlementId` — where the exertion served, never where the record now stands (read from `snapshot.worldState` — the persisted `exertion`, never `aim.exert`: a `faltered` exertion and a duel's `voided` loser add no row; an `indecisive` duel leaves both `exerted`, so both rows apply and cancel), and each domain's stressor gate spreads it into its own `gateResult([...])` list: one line per stressor gate, TEN gates (`religious_conversion_fracture · disease_outbreak · rebellion · insurgency · political_fracture · monster_raider_pressure · market_shock · wartime · siege · infiltration`) plus the import, ≤ 11 lines in `stressorGates.js` (609 → ≤ 620). The product stays clamped to [0.1, 3] by `clampMult`; a champion and a villain in one town multiply and cancel. The row's `reason` IS the felt sentence on scrutiny, and it NAMES the paragon and its calling (the DM's scrutiny surface is truth; the Evidence Law: who, which power, what effect, complete and not cryptic); a court-voiced line withholds the name only where that court does not know it, and says so ("someone is feeding Kell false reports; Kell does not know who"). `<name>`, `<creed>`, `<town>` and `<court>` are filled from the record; nothing is parsed (CH16).
`paragonRows` returns NO row for an exertion that answered a `captive_held` source (§4.5): breaking a jailer's cells neither calms that town's streets (the magistrate's ×0.8 "hears the people's grievances in <town>") nor stirs them (the agitator's ×1.3); the act's one consequence is OP's card and its deed.

| Domain | Champion (display) · Villain (display) | Gate(s) the row enters | Champion row (DRAFT mult · reason) | Villain row (DRAFT mult · reason) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| faith | saint · heretic | `religious_conversion_fracture` | a saint of the town's PATRON creed 0.8 "<name>, a saint of <creed>, shores up the orthodoxy in <town>." · a saint of ANOTHER creed 1.25 "<name>, a saint of <creed>, preaches in <town>'s market." | 1.3 "<name>, a heretic, sows doubt in <creed>'s congregations at <town>." |
| health | doctor · plaguebearer | `disease_outbreak` | 0.75 "<name>, a healer of renown, tends the sick in <town>; fewer outbreaks take hold there." | 1.35 "<name>, a carrier of sickness, has passed through <town>." |
| order | magistrate · agitator | `rebellion` · `insurgency` · `political_fracture` | 0.8 "<name>, a magistrate, hears the people's grievances in <town>." | 1.3 "<name>, an agitator, stirs the streets of <town>." |
| wildland | warden · beast-caller | `monster_raider_pressure` (the AMBIENT raider stressor's birth only — never a menace's held record, §6.2) | 0.75 "<name>, a warden, keeps the wild roads about <town>." | 1.3 "<name>, a beast-caller, draws the beasts down on <town>." |
| trade | merchant prince · speculator | `market_shock` | 0.8 "<name>, a merchant prince, steadies <town>'s market with credit." | 1.3 "<name>, a speculator, is buying up <town>'s stores." |

| war | captain · warlord | `siege` (champion, at the town it holds) · `wartime` (villain, at the court it serves) — both birth rows light with CH-3 wherever `stressorsEnabled` is; the army-record run side waits on C4 | 0.85 "<name>, a captain of renown, holds <town>'s walls." | 1.25 "<name>, a warlord, has <court> spoiling for a fight." |
| information | spymaster · deceiver | `infiltration` | 0.8 "<name>, a master of secrets, keeps watch for <court>." (at its employer) | 1.3 "<name>, a deceiver, is feeding <court> false reports." — in a court's own voice where it does not know him: "someone is feeding the court false reports" |

The magnitudes scale with the paragon's FAME band (RN — the owner's "losing repuation (which would also affect their abilities to a degree)"), every one a DRAFT row on the FP-46 roster (UNMEASURED, requirement 18).

**6.2 The run side — where the domain's truth is computed, one multiplicand, identity when absent.**

| Domain | Seam (`path :: symbol`, host) | The lean | Lit where | If dark |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| faith | `religiousContest.js :: advanceReligionStates` at BOTH strength sites (493; ≤ 6 lines): (3a) an exerting saint is a CARRIER — inside the spread lane's carrier-reach build a leaf `paragonFaithCarriers(worldState)` notes the saint's creed (`creedRef`) as reaching its town with a bounded carrier (DRAFT 0.45, the vassal edge's carrier size), so a creed with no foothold can ENTER through `attemptEntry`; (3b) the present-deity multiplicand `paragonFaithMult(worldState, cid, dref)` on `strengthByRef` | a saint exerting raises ITS creed's strength (DRAFT ×1.15) and carries it in; a heretic lowers the creed it works against — the town's patron unless authored otherwise (DRAFT ×0.87); identity when none. A SWORN heretic's DEFAULT target skips its own god: where the town's patron is the god it is sworn to, it works against no creed there by default; an AUTHORED `creedRef` still names its target — the DM's fallen one, who pays in its flock's regard and its `shame` (§10b); a saint's sworn god IS its `creedRef`. (3a)'s carrier hands `attemptEntry` the WORLD's own snapshot of the god's `_deityRef` wherever any religion state holds one, and the saint's OATH embed (`swornDeityOf`; the oath REQUIRED for a saint, §10b) only where none does — the one place the oath enters world state (CH22) — because the carrier needs the deity object (`religiousContest.js`: "`const { deity, carrier, occupied } = reaching.get(dref);`"). The contest returns early where the faith layer is not active (`religiousContest.js :: advanceReligionStates`: "`if (!isSubsystemActive(snapshot, 'religion')) return …`"), so a saint's oath to a god seated nowhere is admitted as CUSTOM CONTENT WITHOUT A CONTEST (a saint seats no god) | 3b: the local lane in any deity-bearing world (no flag); 3a: `faithSpreadEnabled` (dramatic, full — the reach map is empty when spread is off); unseating 4 presets. (3a)'s saint-oath arm lands WITH CH-11 — before it, (3a) carries only a creed some town's religion state already holds (the degraded arm) — and is DARK until the god holds a seat somewhere or the faith layer is active (`religionActive` — `pulseKernel.js`: "`const religionActive = isSubsystemActive(snapshot, 'religion');`") | without spread the saint tilts only a creed already present; where the faith layer is dark the saint tilts no contest, and its oath still leans its own roll (§10b). The owner's "when they travel to a settlement, they boost their respective religions conversion" needs the entry site as well as the present-deity site: these two sites and the `religious_conversion_fracture` row are CH's three faith carriers, the ones PL §14.7 is to cite |
| health | `pestilenceKernel.js :: advanceSettlementPestilence` — `care01Of` (169; ≤ 2 lines) | `care01 + paragonCareLean(worldState, id)` clamped: a doctor exerting +DRAFT 0.15; a plaguebearer −DRAFT 0.15 — care's REAL reach: the takes-hold roll at a landed front (`spatial/pestilence.js :: onsetProbability`, "MINUS care") and the recovering fade; it does not shorten an active `disease_outbreak` stressor, writes no condition, and never touches the Table Ledger's `plague` obligation (one plague: the fold's row for plague-10.2-7) | `epidemicActive` (spatial canon; on a map-module world the canon is born with the campaign, H22 — canonized ≠ premium, the COMPATIBLE ruling) | the birth row alone (aspatial) |

| wildland | MN's `menace_siege` resolver (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_MN.md` §5.2; verdicts `holds · falls · relieved · lifted`, a breach a receipt inside `holds`) and MN's raid wear (`menaceCounterTerms`, MN §5.5) | DECLARED BY MN: on the SIEGE, a warden whose week's `exertion.outcome` in the besieged town is `exerted` (read through `paragonLean`, never `aim.exert` — MN §5.2 reads it so) multiplies the verdict's keyed draw by `paragonLean(…, 'menace_siege_draw').mult` — a warden's factor `1 + 0.05` toward holding, a beast-caller's `1 − 0.05` toward the breach (DRAFT) — inside MN's clamped draw bias (±0.20 with the DM's tilt): a DRAW BIAS applied as `draw × mult`, never an additive shift and never a re-classification of might or defence; in a RAID, `menaceCounterTerms` row (1): the wear read × `paragonLean(…, 'menace_raid_wear').mult` — a warden whose exertion in the raided town is `exerted` ×1.10, a beast-caller ×0.90 (MN's clamp of the list at ±0.15) — one form, `x × mult`, which MN applies at its §5.2 and §5.5; the course of a menace is the DM's alone (MN1, MN6), so a beast-caller NEVER steers one — nor raises one: "menaces are DM authored only, never organically emergent" (the owner, 01:33; MN12) | the siege term `npcConsequencesEnabled` + `paragonGiftsEnabled` + `menaceStrikesEnabled`; the raid term `npcConsequencesEnabled` + `paragonGiftsEnabled` (with MN's `menacesEnabled`) | lights with MN-2 (C10) — CH registers its two terms in MN's own leaves, the receipts naming the paragon; the domain value is `wildland` (`wilds` is a landed biome display literal, §2.3) |
| war | `warArmyRecord.js :: seedDeploymentState` — `commandQuality` through the generals design's `deployment.general` | the captain as the army's general (or riding with a lower unit: a per-battle term on the same record) | `warLayerEnabled` + `warGeneralsEnabled` (unbuilt) | SLOT C4 (the generals chain); never `armyTransitKernel.js` (798) |
| information | `espionage/espionageMath.js :: covertCompetence01` / the host side of `catchChance01` (`WARINESS_W`); the deceiver's false report = IN's LIE family with its EMPLOYER as `liarId` | a spymaster's missions and counter-intelligence; a deceiver's plants | `espionageActive` (lit nowhere) | SLOT C1 (the statecraft band) |
| trade | EC's `MARKET_CONDITION_BANDS` / EC-13's cornering (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_EC.md`) | supply eased or cornered | unbuilt | SLOT C6 (the economy) |

**SHARED SEAMS.** Two programs lean on the same terms — `strengthByRef` (RN's priest and CH's saint and heretic) and `reserveFor`'s `reserve01` (RN's envoy relief and CH's envoy) — so each is composed through ONE leaf per seam with a joint cap and a fixed order (RN's lean, then CH's), OWNED BY RN (the fold's §3.3.16 owned-unit row: "ONE owned-unit row for the shared lean composers (RN owns; TRUTH)") — a lean on the domain's own physics, so the composers read TRUTH, the leaning exertion being CH's persisted `exertion.outcome`; CH registers its term there and never multiplies a seam alone.

**6.3 The order inside a tick (the latency of each seam, declared).** The stressor gates (`pulseKernel.js` → `ageRoamingStressors(`) and the faith contest (`advanceReligionStates(`) run BEFORE the person stage (`advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse(`), so a paragon that decides in week t to exert is read by those seams in week t+1; the plague front (`advanceSettlementPestilence(`) runs AFTER it and reads the person stage's world, so the care lean applies in the SAME tick; the pact answer and the peace decision read it at their own stage's position (CH-3's car-0 measures each; PLAUSIBLE t+1). FIRST-VISIBLE: the week after it arrives and chooses to exert — the stressor gate row's reason in the stressor's receipt and the paragon's own dossier line ("Sister Aude is preaching in Tolle"). A FAILED exertion (§12) contributes nothing that week (`exertion.outcome: 'faltered'`); a duel's loser is `voided` (§13). EVERY seam — the stressor gate rows, the five run-side leaves (`paragonFaithCarriers`, `paragonFaithMult`, `paragonCareLean`, `paragonPactLean`, `paragonPeaceLean`) and `paragonLean` — reads the exertion AT `exertion.settlementId`, so a lean read at t+1 lands where the paragon exerted at t even if the DM's dark-arm `assign` has since placed it elsewhere (§4.2 (1)).

**6.4 Counterforce and the felt chain.** Each seam's own counterforces stand (the incumbent creed's legitimacy and standing hysteresis, the town's care capacity and the plague's course, the court's posture and the proposer's credibility, the stressor's catalog counterforce rows); the villain on the same seam; the paragon's failure setting; and the weekly choice itself (a mercenary saint unpaid does not preach). The owner's example — the saint supplanting a patron "depending on how long they stay … how big the population is and how diemetrically opposed the current religion's alignment is" — falls out of the landed contest: shares move toward strength each tick by `advanceShares`, the contest's `targetMass` slows a large town, `incumbentCounterForce` resists an opposed creed, and the patron contest (`patronContestOdds`) decides the seat; the saint only tilts its creed's strength while it stays and exerts — and, where spread is lit, carries its creed into a town where it had no foothold (6.2's 3a site).

**Alignment line (requirement 13).** READ: every gift's direction reads the polarity and the target's alignment through RN's inherent value (a doctor's cure is relief on its face; a plaguebearer's spread harm on its face); the saint's targeting reads the creed's recorded axes (`evil01`, `chaos01`) against the town's, and the creeds' relation through `stanceOf`'s consumed fields (`cooperation`, `betrayalHazard`) or RN's creed regard — never `aggression`. WRITE: a paragon moves no settlement's alignment directly; its deeds move constituents only through their existing writers (the faith contest's shares, the stressors, the conditions).

## §7 BOUND AND FREE — RETAINERS BOTH WAYS (CH-4)

**The record.** `paragon.retainer = { employerId, tie, sinceTick, termsBand, covert?: true, exposedAt? }` — `termsBand ∈ meagre · decent · handsome · princely` (census: 0 literals each) (`exposedAt` is P2's latch, §9, named so the two records agree). A BOUND paragon always carries one (its office); a FREE paragon carries one only while a temporary retainer stands. The court is the `employer` in code (never `patron`, a landed word three times over); "hire" and "employment" are prose.

**Free paragons seek work.** A free paragon whose purse is `scraping` or worse, or whose calling has gone unanswered for a season, weighs `seek_work`: the courts it believes have a call in its domain AND fit its values (its `ideal` against the court's ruler's ideal; its creed against the court's patron creed by `stanceOf`'s consumed fields or RN's creed regard — for a paragon of ANY calling "its creed" is its sworn god (`swornDeityOf`, §10b; a faith paragon's `creedRef` already is), read by this same test, and an unsworn paragon of another calling brings no creed term; its alignment against the ruler's — the court's reading is the ruler's three coordinates, `warSeatBooks.js :: rulerAlignment`, made an export by a zero-line change of `function rulerAlignment(` to `export function rulerAlignment(`) OR pay (before an offer: the court's TIER, a public fact on every surface, and — where it has stood there — the court's `prosperityRank.js :: prosperityRank01` as its picture saw it; once offered, the offer's `termsBand`; the court SETS its terms' CEILING from ST's ONE settlement band `settlementFundingBand(worldState, courtId, snapshot)` (itself the cut of `prosperityRank01`, ST §2.4), so a court's purse is read ONCE for its sites, its riders' pay and its retainers — `unfunded` → at most `meagre` · `meagre` → `decent` · `funded` → `handsome` · `lavish` → `princely` (DRAFT, UNMEASURED) — and `treasuryBandOf` where the treasury is lit) — the band half is always on, from the one settlement band, and the coin half is W-COIN's `retainers` row (CH10); if CH lands before ST's leaf, CH's commit lands that pure read in ST's module, ST its owner. Arriving, it offers its service: a `retainer_offer` hearing (§18).

**Courts seek paragons they have heard of** (the owner, message 2: "or are sought out by NPC of a settlement based on belief of where they could be"). A court with a call in a domain at `grave` or worse at home, whose BELIEVED fame of a paragon of that domain (RN) reaches `regional`, and whose regard of it (RN, derived from its members) is `indifference` or better, sends a RECRUITER to where it BELIEVES the paragon is (`believedWhereabouts`, §2.2: where the newest heard deed WHOSE DOER is the paragon took place, by RN's heard test `heardAt(…).heardTick` at the court's town; in the omniscient arm the newest inside whose reach that town lies). THE RECRUITER IS A PRICED JOURNEY, NEVER AN ERRAND ROW — GR-2's landed idiom (`pactProposals.js :: pactTransportFor`: "MINTING THROUGH THE SPINE MEANS PRICING A JOURNEY AND VALIDATING A CLASS, NEVER WRITING A ROW", `emigreErrand.js`): CH's leaf `paragonRecruit.js` registers ONE `ERRAND_CONSUMERS` row (`recruiters`, its class by domain, written as a quoted literal — HZ-LITERALCLASS) and mints through `errandMint.js :: mintErrandSpine` with an injected one-leg plan whose arrival is priced by the one read (`hopWeeks(digest, courtId, atId, season)`, or graph hops at `UNPRICED_LEG_WEEKS`), reading only the mint's `reason` (`spine` ⇒ the card says "riders"; `dark` ⇒ the journey still travels on its priced weeks, as GR-2's `abstract` transport does); its state is ONE row on the SOUGHT paragon's own record, `paragon.soughtBy: { courtId, atId, arrivesAt, termsBand, covert? }` (≤ 2 rows; the oldest yields), so no new ledger key is minted. At `arrivesAt`, if the paragon stands at `atId`: a `retainer_offer` hearing (§18); if it has gone, the row drops with the court's receipt — "Merewick's riders reached Tolle to find the doctor gone north" — and that court sends no second recruiter after the same paragon for a season (keyed on the dropped row's tick; DRAFT, UNMEASURED). The paragon's own choice never reads `soughtBy` before the offer arrives (the row is machinery, not its belief). Two courts may bid: offers arriving in one week share ONE hearing card, and the default takes the better by the paragon's own weights. A CROWN (a polity's root, PL) hires for its lands: a paragon bound to the root moves across the members PL's crown reach covers (§5). Every engine-made offer to a VILLAIN is covert (CH21). TIER: the person tier alone — the recruiter needs no errand row and no lit spine — so the recruiter works in every world where CH is lit, honouring the owner's "sought out by NPC of a settlement based on belief of where they could be" (vetoable: the alternative is the owner's errand-row slot alone, with no recruiter in any world until it lands).

**A bounty is an offer (THE BODIES' item 33, approved by the owner — BOARD, the 18:27 stamp, verbatim: "i like all of these options for what was missing. do those").** A court that POSTS A BOUNTY on a NAMED body (THE BODIES' hearing and its treasury cost — the counterforce; item 32b: only a named body carries one) puts a call on the board (§14). A FREE paragon who KNOWS of it (the board's three roads) and whose own weights take it — a mercenary nature by the bounty's terms, a champion of a hunting calling (`wildland` · `war` · `order`) by the call — weighs `seek_work` toward the posting court with THE BOUNTY AS THE OFFER: its `termsBand` the bounty's, under the same one ceiling every retainer reads (`settlementFundingBand`, above). Arriving, the offer is the court's `retainer_offer` card (§18; a bounty and any other bid that week share one card); `accepted` makes it a TEMPORARY retainer of the posting court ("until a better deal or reason comes along"), and it is then a RETAINED paragon whom the court's `hunt_taken` card may name as the hunt's leader (§4.2 `hunt_body`; item 40's preference — "a RETAINED paragon of the court … a FREE paragon or a CORPS answering the BOUNTY"). A paragon already bound to or retained by the posting court needs no offer. A CORPS answers by the same road and hunts with its OWN unit (PR §5.5: "a CORPS answering a bounty hunts with its OWN unit, never PR's riders"), meeting the body as the war machinery meets one (BD §9.8: a gang scatters to the trail before a column, a pack is fought at its size). The bounty's coin is the treasury's (BD's cost row; W-COIN's) and no person holds coin (CH10): the purse reads the terms as paid. A claimed bounty is ONE `hunt_won` deed (§2.2b). The PARTY answers through PT's mirror of `party_hunted` (PT's).

**The retainer kind `leads` — a paragon at the head of a body (THE BODIES' item 32).** A NAMED gang or conclave ("a leader … may only fill a named body's slot"; "Beasts have no leader") may have ONE leader, a paragon the DM places at its head — DM-authored custom content, in the brief's item 32 that the owner approved (BOARD, the 18:27 stamp, verbatim: "i like all of these options for what was missing. do those"): "the villain who leads the gang, the prophet who leads the conclave". The DM's naming act (THE BODIES' verb "NAME its leader", item 36, on CN's console) writes `paragon.leads: { bodyId, sinceTick }` through CH's leaf (`paragonRetainer.js :: setLeader`) and the ledger's one writer (`moveNpcRecord`); the ENGINE NEVER NAMES A LEADER (CH1; purpose only by the DM, CH3). It sits BESIDE `retainer`, never inside it, so a paragon may serve a court and lead a body at once — a court's villain at the head of the gang that court employs (§9) — and `employerId` stays a court (one word, one meaning). While it leads: its leg MIRRORS the body's (§4.2 (3)); it exerts its gift where the body stands (a prophet preaching in the towns its conclave enters; an agitator stirring the town its gang works), `exertion.settlementId` that town; THE BODIES read its chart for the body's choice weights and its presence for the body's cohesion through `paragonOf` (BD's half — CH supplies the view, never the weights). ONE LEADER RECORD: `paragon.leads` is THE record of a body's leader — THE BODIES store no `leaderRef`; their `bodyLeaderOf(worldState, bodyId)` (BD §2.5) (named so because `leaderOf` bare is a module-local function, `src/domain/worldPulse/urbanFabricKernel.js :: leaderOf(stocks)`, and is avoided) is a READ of this field over `paragonsOf`, memoized per snapshot — and THE BODIES' verb `BODY_LEADER` (install · remove; BD §11) writes THROUGH `setLeader`, the field's one writer; `setLeader` keeps ONE leader per body (naming a second paragon to a body already led drops the first's `leads` in the same write, with a receipt naming both — the DM's word is canon, newest wins), so `bodyLeaderOf` never returns two; and EVERY path that drops `leads` — the DM's removal or the naming act's inverse, the DM's `kill`, a heard `polarity_turn`, the body's end — drops the body's leader in the SAME tick, with no stale copy anywhere. A DM-named leader never steps down by the engine's choice (purpose only by the DM, CH3): CH admits no engine resignation path, and the DM's `BODY_LEADER` remove is the only resignation (vetoable: the alternative adds a weekly step-down choice for a leader whose nature turns against its body). It ENDS by the DM's act (the verb's inverse, or `kill`), by a heard `polarity_turn` (a turned leader leaves its body; the turn's receipt is the shock THE BODIES read, item 35's split), or by the body's end (the field drops at the next pass, with a receipt — and THE BODIES' `bodyLeaderOf` reads none from the tick the body ends, since an ended body's id names no living body); a leader TAKEN by an enemy is custody, which waits on ES-2b (§12.4). Towns know the leader only by BELIEF — THE BODIES' belief record of the body ("the leader if known", item 45), never this field. TIER: `paragonRetainersEnabled` + THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled`; dark ⇒ the field is never written. `leads` is a sibling field, never a `kind` on the retainer: one retainer slot would forbid a court's villain from leading the gang its court employs, and `employerId` would mean a court in one row and a body in another (vetoable: the alternative is `retainer.kind ∈ serves · leads` with one tie per paragon).

**The answer.** The paragon's default answer scores the retainer (terms, values, the calling's need there, its relations to the court) against its best alternative plus `COMMIT_MARGIN`; a mercenary nature weighs terms, a loyal or devout one values; an offer that would cross its `neverLine` (a court of a rival creed for `never_serve_rival_creed`; a covert retainer for `never_take_covert_work`) is refused — or, when its pull is above the line's resistance (the `defining` position's strength), becomes a `never_line_tested` hearing instead (§13). A great contract (`princely` terms) is a big personal decision and is always shown as the offer's hearing.

**Pay — terms, not coin (CH10).** The retainer's `termsBand` is what the court offers and the paragon weighs; no coin moves today. The treasury's one upkeep law (`treasury.js :: UPKEEP_LEDGERS`: "A widening car adds the member and its call site in the same act") cannot reach a retainer without a kernel argument — `advanceTreasury(sieged, { interval, tick, deployment, blockade, rules })` takes no `worldState`, its one caller is the frozen kernel, and retainers live in `spatialLedgers.npcLedger` — so the SLOT is a NEW `UPKEEP_LEDGERS` row `retainers` in the treasury's own module WHEN W-COIN LIGHTS, beside ST's `sites`, a W-COIN inbox row; its leaf-first car carries the court's retainer roll on the settlement record `advanceTreasury` already receives (the person stage's `settlementUpdates`), measured at that unit (PLAUSIBLE). Until then the purse reads the terms as paid (`treasuryEnabled` is lit nowhere today, so this is every world's arm); no person holds coin and no transfer primitive is called (`computeCoinTransfer` pays only settlement→settlement).

**Breaking and resigning.** A free paragon leaves a temporary retainer when a candidate beats staying by `COMMIT_MARGIN`; that is a DEED (RN: the employer's regard falls with the reason "broke faith"; future employers who hear of it trust it less) and never a hearing (nothing between two courts is at stake). A BOUND paragon that would leave RESIGNS: a `paragon_resigns` hearing (`resigns · stays`, default by its choice); resigning makes it free, writes the deed, and the record stays in `roamers` (its retainer ends or becomes a free one). If the employer is razed or removed, the retainer ends with a receipt and the paragon is free; if the employer falls to conquest, the paragon chooses afresh (it may serve the fallen lord's court where its vassals `stand_by` — PL §5.7 — or go free; `legitimateRemnantOf` is the landed faction's, never CH's word) — a weekly choice, not a hearing.

**Kept distinct from the force market — which now closes into THE CORPS.** A retainer of a PERSON hires ONE named person: no strength number, no census movement, never `mercenaryMarket.js :: mercSupplementOf`. A named company for hire is THE CORPS (§16b): the owner's "Mercenaries are fine as is. And effectively can be emulated or used for our champion system just replace a champion with a corp" closes WC §1.12.5 (e)'s parked force market into this volume; `mercSupplementOf` stays untouched ("fine as is").

A court's retainer OFFER to a corps takes ONE bounded PRIOR lean of that court's way of war (THE WAY OF WAR's ask of CH, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_WS.md` §4.2 C9 and §13: "the retainer offer | CH §7 | one lean under `way_coalition`/`way_raiding`/`way_attrition`; the corps unchanged") — `wayLeanOf` at WS's ONE bound (`WS_LEAN_W`, a factor in [0.88, 1.12], ×1 exactly when dark) under `way_coalition`, `way_raiding` (plunder) and `way_attrition`, entering the offering court's own offer score in CH's paragon stage at WS-3 (C9, at this volume's CH-4 unit); the CORPS itself is unchanged — born only by the DM (§16b).

**Felt.** "Captain Vey has taken Merewick's coin." · "Sister Aude refused Kell's offer: she will not serve a court of the Ash." · "Merewick's riders reached Tolle to find the doctor gone north." SCRUTINY: the terms band, the offer's three reasons. Clock: weekly; an offer's hearing is its arrival week; FIRST-VISIBLE: the offer's card. Counterforce: values against pay; a rival bid; a broken retainer's cost in trust.

## §8 THE LINE HOME — THE HANDLER AT THE EDGE (CH-7)

No errand row can name a paragon at master (§0), so a paragon abroad runs no errand and walks no errand leg, and `counterIntelSweep.js :: resolveSweep` sweeps only "the covert watchers standing on the town, the covert errands at its gate" — ES records, never a paragon's own leg. The line home therefore rests on what exists — the DM's direction and the paragon's own leg; the court-sent arm waits on the owner's errand-row slot (below).

**The owner's handlers.** A BOUND paragon POSTED ABROAD — sent there by the DM's `assign` (the DM's direction: the owner's "they have no purpose or destiny unless directed by the user"), its arrival outside its employer's lands recorded as `paragon.postId` (written by the paragon pass the week a bound paragon placed by an assignment stands outside its lands; cleared when the DM assigns it home, when it resigns, or when its retainer ends) — a spymaster, a deceiver, a provocateur, an agitator, an envoy — keeps a RENDEZVOUS with a LIAISON from home ("handler" in prose) every `RENDEZVOUS_WEEKS` (DRAFT 8, UNMEASURED) at the meeting place: the member of its employer's lands nearest its post by `hopWeeks` (graph hops aspatially) — the owner's "meet at the edge of their territory". It walks there and back on ITS OWN LEG (the aim `keep_rendezvous`; its post counts as inside its reach for this aim only). The liaison is the home court's officer at that town — the court's word, not a second traveller. At the meeting: orders and the TRUE state of home in (the paragon's `carried` facts refreshed from the home court's own belief; its `picture` refreshed), its findings out — never by a write into the home court's belief (beliefs have one writer): the meeting seeds ONE telling about its post — the `findings` row of RN's `TELLING_SOURCES` (RN §3.4), read at the NEXT tick's rumour stage: for each paragon with a `postId` whose `aim` is `keep_rendezvous` and which stands at `aim.destId` (it arrived there in last tick's person stage), one seed with `eventRef` `rendezvous:<wnpcId>:<aim.sinceTick>` (one telling per trip; the prefix is CH's, named here as RN asked), hop-0 origin the EMPLOYER'S SEAT (`originIds: [<employerId>]` — the liaison carries the report home), ARRIVING when the liaison could carry it there — `eventTick` the meeting's tick and `arrivalTick` = the seed's tick + (the meeting town = the seat ? 0 : `Math.max(1, finiteNumber(hopWeeks(digest, meetingId, seatId, season), 1))`), RN's form verbatim (RN §3.4: "the relay's OWN price from the meeting town to the seat — 0 when they are one town … else the relay's `travelTicks` line exactly"), the relay's own line in master's `spatial/rumorNetwork.js :: advanceRumorLedgers` ("`const travelTicks = Math.max(1, finiteNumber(weeks, 1));`"), so an unreachable or absent pair (`hopWeeks` returns `null`) and the aspatial arm (no digest) carry ONE week — never "at once" (a bare sum `tick + null` is `tick`: EXECUTED, `cure/D-CH-r5-probe/arrivalForm.out`) and never a second speed for news — and the report travels home at the ONE speed of news and person (a record with `arrivalTick` above the tick is IN TRANSIT, "invisible to the read model until it arrives" — `rumorNetwork.js`'s arrival-queue idiom; `beliefMap.js :: reportsBySubject`: "`if (arrivalTick > now) continue; // in transit — not yet heard`"; and "In-transit records … never age", so its 13-week life counts from the arrival) (EXECUTED against master's `advanceRumorLedgers`, `cure/D-CH-r4-probe/findingsArrival.out`: stamped with a four-week journey the record is held by `home` alone, first readable at tick 14 with the seed at 10 and gone at 28, 13 weeks after its arrival; stamped with no journey, readable at 10 and gone at 24; the unstamped control still relays to `meet` and `third`), the CONTINUATION GUARD PRE-STAMPED (`relayedTick` = the seed's tick, so `rumorNetwork.js :: advanceRumorLedgers`'s relay skips it: "`if (record.relayedTick != null) continue; // continuation guard`") — so the report lives in the home court's ledger alone, no other court ever holds it, and `scope: 'settlement'` is honoured by construction — and DECLARED `significance: 'notable'` (its TTL 13 weeks, `RUMOR_TTL_TICKS`) and `score: 0` (read only by a `major`'s hop budget, `maxHopsFor`), and DECLARED CONTENT `what: 'paragon_findings'`, `whereId: <postId>` and `partyIds: [<postId>]` (so `reportsBySubject` reads it as a report about the post — the one belief road by which the home court's picture of the post refreshes), `scope: 'settlement'`, `magnitude: 0`, `causeClass` and `deityName` null, `visibility: 'mechanical'` (an operative's report is never a rumour panel's line) and `paragonId`. Beliefs have one writer, and this is it; in the omniscient arm the court already knows. PLAUSIBLE until CH-7's car-0 measures the row's inputs on `worldState` at the rumour stage. The pre-stamped guard is EXECUTED (`cure/D-CH-r3-probe/findingsHome.out`): stamped, only `home` ever holds the record; unstamped, `home`, `meet` and `third` (two hops) do, so every court within two hops would refresh its belief of the post from another court's operative; a `notable`, score-0 record's hop budget is 2, and it drops when more than 13 weeks have passed since its arrival. RN's telling arm copies a row's declared `relayedTick` onto the seed (RN's half).

**Where it can be caught.** The meeting is in the operative's HOME territory, where the target's counter-intelligence has no reach. What the target CAN catch is the operative's own RE-ENTRY: each return to its post is an arrival at the target's town's gate (`gateDefault`, §11), so a watch that mistrusts it or believes it a villain admits it only `admitted_under_watch` — the hearing — and ES's host-side wariness (`WARINESS_W`) rises for the stay where ES is lit; on the road, a patrol that notices it (THE PATROLS, when lit — §5's patrols line) turns it back. The meeting itself is exposable only by a third court's watchers standing at the meeting town (ES, when lit) or by a corruption-web asset (a mole) inside the home court. A turned-back or refused operative EXPOSES as §9's road 3 (a caught run) and does not reach its post; HOLDING it waits on ES-2b's custody wave — nothing here designs on it.

**Envoys and saints.** An envoy paragon posted at a foreign court keeps the line by the same rendezvous (the errand family's dispatches wait on the owner's slot with the court's errand). A saint BOUND to a temple court (its employer) refreshes at its MOTHER HOUSE — the employer's seat — whenever it stands there (it is `placed` there, CH9, and the landed home read counts it home); a free saint has no line.

**The court-sent arm — the owner's slot.** A court SENDING its own bound operative abroad on its own initiative (an errand), and a liaison who travels to meet it, need a persisted errand row that can name a paragon: THE OWNER'S errand-row slot (CH8). When it lands, the operative is claimed `placed` at its seat (CH9), its legs are covert errand rows the target's sweep sees at its gate, and the meeting refresh rides the errand's home delivery (WR-7a's physical message).

**Tier.** The posted operative: the person tier (`npcConsequencesEnabled`) + `paragonCovertEnabled`; the watch's wariness ES (`counterIntelActive`, the statecraft chain C1); patrols PR. Degraded arm (no DM post, or covert dark): no rendezvous; the operative's picture refreshes only where it stands; its findings reach home only when it goes home. FIRST-VISIBLE: the week a re-entry is refused or watched ("Kell's watch turned back a stranger on the south road; the trail led to Merewick"). Counterforce: the target's watch and wariness; patrols; the operative's own notoriety by office (`operativeNotoriety01`: "a notorious spy is easier to expose"); its fame (RN — a famous face is known at the town's gate).
