## §16 THE PARTY — CHAMPIONS COME TO MEET THE PARTY (CH-6, joint with PT)

The party volume (`DESIGN_FP_ARCH_PT.md`) owns the party-side card (`party_visit` and the purposeful party kinds) and the party's presence; CH owns the paragon's CHOICE and two marks.
- **With a reason** (the owner: "employment or history or relationship or regard"): a bound paragon carrying its employer's summons, offer or threat (the office that owns the act — party brief §5), or a paragon with history, an authored or emergent relation, or regard at `esteem`/`adoration` or `enmity`/`hatred` toward the party, weighs `visit_party` like any purpose; the resulting card is PT's request, offer, warning, challenge or hostile attempt.
- **Curiosity alone** ("Just for meeting"): it must BELIEVE where the party stands (PT's ONE whereabouts read `whereaboutsOf(party)` — the later of the DM's `presentAt` and `lastHeard` — as heard where the paragon stands) and be CLOSE (`within_a_month` by `reachBandOf`, graph hops aspatially); it is drawn by alignment and personality (§4.4: curious, proud — HUMILITY vice, "to measure themselves against the party", and a villain's courteous call on a famous hero). It is RARE (a keyed hash at a DRAFT low base), COOLS (`marks.curiosityAt` and a DRAFT year's cooldown), and NEVER repeats once met (`marks.metParty`, one-way — set by CH's stage, the field's one writer, when PT's `met_in_person` deed, minted by PT's door at `RECORD_MEETING` (PT §7.3: "doer the party, target the person"), names this paragon as its target).
- **The default** of every party card is PT's: nothing has happened to the party yet (requirement 24, restated). Once met at the table, RN's regard link forms from what it had heard, adjusted by what happened (the DM may set it), and drives what it does next.

---

## §16b THE CORPS — A FREE COMPANY CARRIES THE MARK (CH-10)

The owner's word, verbatim: "Mercenaries are fine as is. And effectively can be emulated or used for our champion system just replace a champion with a corp" — ruled as THE CORPS (OD-CH-36).

**What it is.** The paragon's mark may sit on a free COMPANY as well as on a person: `authored.holderKind: person · corps`. A corps is the WAR calling (`domain: war`) of either side, FREE (for hire) by default or BOUND to a court, and it lives exactly as a paragon does — the weekly choice ("until a better deal or reason comes along"), retainers both ways (§7), fame, regard and deeds (RN), bans and the town's gate (§11), the hearings (§18) — with three differences: it is a UNIT, not a person; it never holds custody; and its strength is a band its unit carries.

**Its record — WC's free unit, never a new ledger.** The mark is ONE conditional, drop-when-empty `paragon` field on WC's free-unit record (`spatialLedgers.freeUnits[unitKey]`, `DESIGN_FP_ARCH_WC.md` §1.7.2: `{ blocks, position, state, supplyBand, driftBand, beliefStaleness }`, `FREE_UNIT_STATES` `operating · foraging · raiding · dissolving`), normalized by the same leaf `normalizeParagon` in WC's free-unit normalizer, landed in the commit of its first writer (CH2's law, for a second host). "No parallel NPC system" holds for both kinds: a corps is WC's unit, a person the ledger's. MOVEMENT AND BATTLE RIDE WC's UNIT UNION — the free unit's own army-style stepper and WC's battles; a captain paragon who rides with it mirrors its leg (the co-location read, CH8). Its weekly choice sets the unit's destination through WC's one stepper (one physics; the corps adds no speed) — the stepper's DESTINATION INPUT, which WC's free units lack today (they steer by WC §1.7.3's rejoin rule), is WC's to add at WC-10 by the fold's §3.1 WC INBOX row (14).

**Born only by the DM.** A corps is DM-authored (custom content; the `introduce` door with `holderKind: corps`), NEVER engine-born: WC's fission products stay unmarked free units, and the engine never promotes one (CH1). Its PEOPLE are never conjured: they come from the census of the origin settlement the DM names, through a WC-registered, DM-authority people-ledger event (one manpower truth; WC's conservation walker judges it) — a WC inbox row, since WC's free units are born only by fission today: THE FOLD'S §3.1 WC INBOX ROW (14) names, AT WC-10, the four WC halves the corps rides — the mark's arm in WC's free-unit normalizer, the DM-authority birth SOURCE in WC's conservation walker (beside its declared `dm_removed` sink, so a DM-born company is a named event and never a hole in the identity), the stepper's destination input, and `removeFreeUnit` as the disband door (below). Its strength band is WC's band of its blocks. A MERCENARY INSTITUTION — an end a gang may reach (THE BODIES' item 42 (b), and the owner's correction, BOARD, the 18:45 stamp, verbatim: "when i mentioned mercenaries, i meant as establishing a merecenary institution where approprate not become an irregular") — is a TOWN'S INSTITUTION, never a corps: it hires out through the force market's road (WC §1.12.5 (e), whose birth path item 42 (b) names), carries no paragon mark, and the engine never promotes it into one (CH1); THE CORPS is the DM-authored, named company that may be such an institution's finest expression (item 42 (b): "the CHAMPION-GRADE company — the named CORPS paragon with a captain and fame — stays DM-AUTHORED custom content … never produced by the engine"). ONE OWNER PER PIECE: the INSTITUTION's birth is THE BODIES' (a gang's `free_company` end, through the one institution writer); its HIRE is the LANDED market, `mercenaryMarket.js` (W-C2: "Where local mercenary / adventurer-guild institutions exist (the catalog SUPPLY), that shortfall becomes DEMAND they answer" — its own town's shortfall, read through `mercSupplementOf`, which WC rules UNTOUCHED: "THE SUPPLEMENT IS UNTOUCHED … Hiring FOREIGN force remains a capability read, exactly as it is today"), WC's and the owner's "Mercenaries are fine as is"; the CROSS-COURT hire of a named company is CH's THE CORPS alone (OD-CH-36), so the force market that closed into CH is the NAMED company's; an institution CONTRACTING ABROAD would be the parked force market built for institutions — its own wave if it is ever unparked, in WC's words "an owner-gated widening, not a WC repair" — never CH's door, and BD's "closed into CH" lines are BD's to re-point.

**Hire without merger.** A corps hires out at the price of trust (its retainer's `termsBand`, §7) and NEVER joins the hirer's manpower pool: its blocks stay its own, so the hirer's census, levy and `mercSupplementOf` are untouched ("Mercenaries are fine as is"). Retained, it marches beside or ahead of the hirer's host; its GIFT is the war calling's lean at ITS OWN strength band — the §6.2 war row on the unit record (its own quality in WC's battle read) and, attached to the hirer's army, the captain's per-battle term on that army record — SLOT C4 (the generals design) with WC's free units.

**Failure, death and custody.** A corps' failure ladder is `setback` (the lean does not apply) · `stumble` (fame dips) · `desertion` (its strength band falls one, the shed blocks taking WC's own road for shed men — dissolution, or, where THE BODIES are lit and WC-11/12's `brigand` tag event exists, THE BODIES' `deserted` birth through that ONE event, BD §3.2 (d), never a CH birth; a corps exists only where WC-10..12 are lit, this section's tier, so the corps' deserters always have WC's road and never meet BD's "where WC's ledger is dark" arm — which waits on WC-11/12 in any case: "Birth (d) is DARK until WC-11/12 lands") · `sell-out` — the top rung: it breaks its retainer and turns on its employer, a `sold_out` deed (§2.2b's act table, villainous on its face, so the villain side's consequences follow — bans, the scandal of regard, no court's trust), heard as `paragon_disgraced` with the corps' sentence on the card (`disgraced` = the sale stands; `weathered` = the DM spares it). A lost battle is WC's (casualties lower its band). NO CUSTODY ROAD: a company is defeated or disbanded, never held. DEATH = DISBANDMENT, THE DM'S: the DM's own act ends it — `disband` IS WC's DM door `removeFreeUnit` (WC §7.D, WC-11) with its ABSORB-TO-NEAREST disposition ("a `shed`, conserved": the company's people re-enter the census at the nearest host, never the `dm_removed` sink), offered on the corps' page beside `introduce` for `holderKind: corps`; the mark leaves with the unit record, and the verb's undo (WC's store-action lifecycle) brings both back (PLAUSIBLE until WC-11's car-0) (never WC's `dissolving`, a free-unit STATE WC enters by its own rules, with no DM path into it); the engine never disbands a marked company — a corps whittled to nothing stands on its page with no men until the DM decides.

**Who it is to the world.** Its actor ref is `{ kind: 'corps', id: unitKey }`: cross-volume rows, each its owner's — SP-4a's `POSTURE_ACTOR_KINDS` and the fold's actor-ref convention gain `corps` — SIX kinds, `settlement · person · party · menace · corps · body` (THE BODIES' `body`, its `bodyKind` a field): a company decides, a body decides, a menace's course is the DM's authored mind (MN12, MN6), and a site does not decide: `site` is a WHERE and a subject, never an actor kind; the exclusion edge's actor-ref widening carries a company's `u:<unitKey>` (PT's shape 3; CH writes the edge); RN keys its fame and regard as the UNIT key `u:<unitKey>` beside `p:<wnpc>` — never `c:`, the court body's prefix (RN's registration; RN's `doer` gains `corps`); The fold carries these rows. The refused-pair rule does not apply to `u:`, so a corps holds regard of the court that hires it and is regarded by it — the relations term (§4.4) and the retainer's answer (§7) read both. Where a company HEARS a tale and reads a deed (RN's second reading for a `u:` holder) is its retaining court's seat, else, free, its unit record's own position. ON THE ROADS (THE PASSAGE): a RETAINED corps — one carrying a `retainer`, bound or free under a temporary one — crossing foreign land is PS's passage mover, its employer's unit (PS1 (d); key `corps:<unitKey>`, PS §2.1); a FREE corps meets each town through CH's town's gate (the corps arm, §11). Its name ("the Grey Company") is the DM's, quoted, never parsed (CH16).

**Felt.** "The Grey Company has taken Merewick's coin and marches for Kell." · "The Grey Company sold Merewick out at the ford; Kell's banners rode through." · "Half the Grey Company slipped away in the night." SCRUTINY: its band, terms, three reasons.

**Tier.** `paragonCorpsEnabled` over `paragonsEnabled` + WC's `freeCompaniesEnabled` (WC-10..WC-12, unbuilt — WC's status of record lists only WC-0A..WC-0E built dark) + `warLayerEnabled`; until WC-10 lands, a corps cannot be introduced (the DM's door offers `person` only). DEGRADED ARM: none — no corps. FIRST-VISIBLE: the corps' first retainer card. This closes WC §1.12.5 (e)'s parked force market into CH (the owner's word); the fold's WC inbox carries the rows ((12) the market's closing; (14) the corps' four WC halves).

## §17 COST — A FEW CHARACTERS DECIDING WEEKLY

Per weekly tick: P paragons × C candidates. Candidates are bounded per paragon: the settlements within `within_a_season` reach (or three graph hops), the top eight calls of its domain, its home, its employer, its nemesis, the party — DRAFT caps. Every per-settlement fact is read through a memo (the call board once per observer per snapshot; beliefs per pass), so the stage is O(P × C) over memoized reads (R1 Q5: the unmemoized ceiling "about 309 ms per tick at 14 settlements … memoized per snapshot it costs about 1.7 ms"). THE COST PIN (CH-9): a 25-paragon world on a 60-settlement seed adds under a DRAFT 5 ms per tick at the stage (UNMEASURED; the owner signs the measured row). A world with no paragon pays one `paragonsOf` empty read. THE DEED-RATE PIN (CH-9): at most DRAFT four `paragon_exerted` deeds per paragon-year on the cost pin's seed (one per stay in its calling), UNMEASURED. The hunts, the ways, the leaders and the bounties add no per-candidate cost: a `hunt_body` aim reads PR's one prediction once a week for its one quarry (PR's memo), a way is a known edge of the ONE ladder (BD's `hiddenWayEdges`, memoized per digest and route-ledger version, ∩ BD's `knownTrailsOf`), a leader reads its body's place, and a bounty is one more call on the memoized board.

**The three choices** (requirement 24): in the hearing's week the DM may leave it to the engine, TILT it (slight · heavy, for either side) or give the VERDICT; the next advance resolves it first from the seal or the default. Month, season and year advances run every CH hearing on its default without stopping, each leaving its receipt ("resolved by the engine's default verdict"). Nothing queues: a duel, a town's-gate card or an offer not answered in its week is resolved, and next week's card (if the engagement still presses) shows it as it now stands.

**Participation on every card it touches.** A paragon present in a settlement that is party to any registered engagement (a siege, a pact acceptance, a `menace_siege`, a town's gate) is named on that card with its week's aim, in the world's words: "Captain Vey is in Kell and has not taken up arms." (a captain present at a besieged town that chose not to exert — the owner's example; the card states the deed, never a forecast). A one-line slot on the card — the card's `participation` column, ONE column the fold's §3.4 names for every registered card (the fold's), filled by CH's stage from `paragonsAt` and `aim`; no card changes its verdict because of the line.

**The champion arm of THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` card (item 40; the card and its kind are THE BODIES', PR §5.5: "registered by its owning volume … never PR").** The card's leader is, by preference, the court's PUBLIC-SECURITY office-holder (item 40 (1): L-BD 23's government-type read — THE BODIES' and PL's, never CH's — save that a PARAGON holds that office only by the DM's appointment, which CH's `securityOfficeOf` reads and THE BODIES' read (1)(i) calls, BD §9.11); CH's ARM supplies preference (2), "a RETAINED paragon of the court", where that office is empty or the DM asks for a champion: CH's leaf `paragonHunt.js :: championForHunt(worldState, courtId, bodyId, tick) → wnpcId | null` names the court's bound or retained paragon of a hunting calling (`wildland` · `war` · `order`, the champion side) whose OWN weekly choice WOULD take `hunt_body` against that body this week WERE IT NAMED — the HYPOTHETICAL choice: `weeklyChoice` scored with `hunt_body` admitted for that one quarry, because §4.2 admits the purpose only once the paragon is named and the leaf asks the question the naming would make true (CH4: action is a choice — a cowardly one declines, a mercenary one weighs the terms; a warden first, `isWardenParagon`, the owner's "prefernce given to wardens and nobles or whowever is in charge of public security"), else `null`; preference (3), a FREE paragon, enters only by answering the court's bounty (§7: the bounty is the offer, so it is retained before it leads). The DM may NAME any paragon or STOP the hunt (requirement 24). The named leader's aim is `hunt_body` with `aim.bodyId` the quarry (§4.2), THE PATROLS' detachment carries it as `leaderRef` (PR §2.5), and the outcome words are the card's — the body's and the unit's, never the person's (item 40: "THE LEADER NEVER DIES BY THE ENGINE"); a win is ONE `hunt_won` deed, minted at the card's close by CH's door with the leader the card names as its doer — a paragon, a corps or a non-paragon office-holder — and THE PATROLS' clash under a led hunt mints none (§2.2b), an escape the failure ladder (`paragon_disgraced`, above) and the body's own `escaped` deed (RN's).

**Scope.** These are engagements between a court and a person (or two persons in a town); OD-CH-3 extends requirement 24's scope to them by delegation, as OD-MN-8 did for menaces (the owner's own words cover them: "Same with NPCs in other settlements or with other settlement's NPCs for any act of foreign policy"). Every row uses the one actor-ref convention `{kind, id?}`; the proposal ring's `targetSaveId` is always a real settlement (the court or the town), with the person on a `subject` dial (the MN convention). WHERE: every row names its place kind (ENC-HL-13; the third host ruling) — `settlement` for every CH kind, a `site` arm from ST's `worldSites`, and the road (`road{a, b}`, PR's one stretch key) for a patrol's turn-back at `person_at_the_gate` where PR is lit, for a body's turn-back and for the attempt on a warden (`road (a, b)`, a way named on the card — never a sixth WHERE kind); `border_segment` is never CH's.

## §19 THE FLAGS (virtual; absent-when-dark; read `=== true`; each lands with its certification row and its lit-mutant pins)

| Flag | Gates | Host flags | May light in |
|---|---|---|---|
| `paragonsEnabled` | the `paragon` field's normalizer arm reads it as data always (so a lit→dark world keeps the field); the stage, the weekly choice, the road, moods, habits, the picture, conditions, the dossier page, the map piece, the DM's `introduce`, the call board, turns, lines, arcs | `npcConsequencesEnabled` (the ledger); fame, regard and deeds `renownEnabled` (RN; without it fame reads `unknown`, regard `indifference`, no deed is minted and gifts run at their base magnitude); the town's-gate renown step `renownLeansEnabled` (RN's notoriety projection, RN §19); the drift arm `characterDriftEnabled`; legs a canonized map (else graph hops; on a map-module world the canon is born with the campaign, H22); `hearingWeekEnabled` for every pause (else defaults resolve in-tick); the trail arm THE BODIES' `hiddenWaysEnabled` on the ONE ladder (its rungs moved by the route lifecycle, `routeLifecycleEnabled` — dark, THE BODIES' candidates at the landed price); the purpose `hunt_body`, THE BODIES' `hunt_taken` card's champion arm and its `hunt_won` THE BODIES' `bodyEffectsEnabled` (the hunt card's flag) with THE PATROLS' `patrolsEnabled` (the detachment, the one prediction) — the bounty and the hunt led light with `bodyEffectsEnabled` (BD §13; RN §3.1 gates `hunt_won` on it), while `leads`, the leader and a body's road arm stay `bodiesEnabled` | where `npcConsequencesEnabled` is lit: dramatic, full at C1's unit (OD-FC-39); the default preset is a later lighting act (OD-CH-4) |
| `paragonGiftsEnabled` | the birth rows, the run-side leans, duels, failure | `paragonsEnabled`; `stressorsEnabled`; faith spread `faithSpreadEnabled`; care `epidemicActive`; pacts `pactFormationEnabled`; the menace arms `menaceStrikesEnabled` (the siege term, MN §5.2) and `menacesEnabled` (the raid term, MN §5.5); peace the peace road (`peaceEngineEnabled` ∧ `warTerminationEnabled`); war `warLayerEnabled` + the generals' flags; information `espionageActive` | with `paragonsEnabled` |
| `paragonRetainersEnabled` | bound and free, offers both ways, the recruiter, resignations, the upkeep sink | `paragonsEnabled`; the recruiter: a PRICED JOURNEY with no errand row (GR-2's idiom, §7) — `errandSpineEnabled` changes only its transport word; pay: none (terms read as paid until W-COIN's `retainers` row, §7); the retainer kind `leads` THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled`; a BOUNTY's answer (its call on the board) `bodyEffectsEnabled`; the DM's office appointment (`heldOffice`, read by `securityOfficeOf`) carries weight only where THE BODIES' office read is lit (`bodiesEnabled`; its ways `hiddenWaysEnabled`) | with `paragonsEnabled` |
| `paragonGateEnabled` | the town's gate for persons, emergent bans, the DM's `banish`, the crown's ban, `banished_found` | `paragonsEnabled`; belief reads a belief info mode; the crown's ban PL's `polityHoldEnabled` (the hold and `crownReachAt`) and its harbouring row `polityEnabled` (a REQUESTED row, written under this flag ∧ `polityEnabled`); the WATCH and the BENCH persons lit (else the ruler); a patrol's turn-back `patrolsEnabled`; a body's turn-back THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled` (on a trail `hiddenWaysEnabled`); the attempt on a warden `bodiesEnabled` | with `paragonsEnabled` |
| `paragonCovertEnabled` | the line home, the covert retainer, leaks, `paragonBeliefs`, the exposure blowback | `paragonRetainersEnabled`; `beliefsActive`; the posted operative's own leg (§8 — the court-sent arm waits on the owner's errand-row slot); the watch's wariness `counterIntelActive`; blowback `corruptionWebEnabled` + its credibility arm `infoStatecraftEnabled`; confirm `espionageActive`; a BODY's tie in the one idiom (§9) THE BODIES' `bodiesEnabled` (its sponsorship and carriers BD's own flags) | with the statecraft band (C1) |
| `paragonCorpsEnabled` | THE CORPS (§16b): the mark on a free unit, its introduction, its weekly choice, its retainers, its gift at its own band, its ladder (`desertion · sell-out`) | `paragonsEnabled` + `paragonRetainersEnabled`; WC's `freeCompaniesEnabled` (WC-10..WC-12, unbuilt) + `warLayerEnabled`; the actor kind `corps` (the fold's SP-4a row) | with WC-10's chain |

Every flag is premium (the simulator is gated for premium; no free-tier arm). All dark ⇒ byte-identical on every seed: the normalizer arm writes nothing on a record without the field (the ledger dormancy golden `tests/property/npcLedgerDormancyGolden.test.js` stands), the stage returns its input by reference, the stressor gate rows return nothing. THE PATRON DEITY adds NO flag: its lean rides `paragonsEnabled` (§7's values term `paragonRetainersEnabled`), its creed's step `paragonGateEnabled`, its flock route RN's `renownEnabled`; with no sworn god every arm is identity, so S1 stands at six flags (vetoable, OD-CH-52).

**TIER lines, the whole volume:** PERSON TIER — `npcConsequencesEnabled` (dramatic, full at C1's unit; dark elsewhere until a lighting act); PREMIUM. BELIEF — fame, regard, the town's gate and the picture read a belief info mode; under `infoMode: 'full'` (full_simulation) RN's tales mutate; in the two news-only presets the parts travel without weathering; in the default and three others (no rumour road) the OMNISCIENT ARM applies — every court knows a paragon's deeds at once inside RN's projected scope, every town's gate judges the truth, the picture is the truth, and no covert secret leaks (§9). FAITH ARMS — `faithSpreadEnabled` (dramatic, full) for the spread; the local lane in any deity-bearing world. HEARINGS — `hearingWeekEnabled` (the pause); defaults in-tick without it. WAR ARMS — `warLayerEnabled`. PACT ARMS — `pactFormationEnabled`. ERRAND ARMS — NO errand row can name a paragon at master (§0), so OD-FC-39's unit lights none of them: the RECRUITER travels as a priced journey wherever CH is lit (§7); the posted operative's line home walks its own leg (§8); the court's errand abroad and the liaison's run wait on THE OWNER'S errand-row slot, their degraded arm every world's arm until it lands. THE CANON — legs, the care lean and the direction words read the world canon; on a map-module world the canon is born with the campaign (H22), so the spatial arm is the default there; canonized ≠ premium — the COMPATIBLE ruling (the world canon is geography, the premium gate sits on the simulator's lanes; requirement 17 carries it in the fold) — and CH, premium in every flag, simply does not run on a free campaign, canonized or not. LEGS read `hopWeeks(…, season)` over the canon's sea lanes and seasonal overlay (§5). CUSTODY — NONE in the engine: it waits on ES-2b's custody WAVE (open by delegation, the wave order standing — no longer an owner gate); the DM's hand (`authored.heldAt`) stays the only hold everywhere CH is lit until it lands (§12.4). SITES — ST's `worldSitesEnabled` for the site rows; PATROLS — `patrolsEnabled` for the patrol rows (§5); the host's patrol facts go out through the window (PR §13), H16 untouched. THE DEFAULT PRESET (`realistic_regional`): CH is dark there while `npcConsequencesEnabled` is; lighting the person substrate in the default preset is an OWNER LIGHTING ROW on the FP-46 roster — every born world changes (OD-CH-4). The owner's word on the default preset, BOARD, verbatim: "i leave to your recommendation" — ruled by delegation on that word: "THE DEFAULT PRESET becomes the BELIEF preset once C0 lights, omniscience kept as a switch". When that act lands, the default's arms are CH's BELIEF arms (the leak, the accusation, the town's gate judging what it has heard, the call board by its three roads), never the omniscient arm named above; CH is still dark there until OD-CH-4's owner lighting row, and the omniscient arm stays whole behind the switch.

## §20 THE WAVES (every charter carries requirement 16/17's lines, L10's EDITOR line, requirement 13's Alignment line, and `causedBy` on every child receipt — requirement 20)

**Where CH sits — coherence chain C12 · THE CHAMPIONS.** After C0 (the hearing week) and C0.5 (the cause walk), after C1 (the statecraft band, whose lighting unit lights `npcConsequencesEnabled`), and after C11 · THE RENOWN (CH reads RN's fame, regard and deeds); before the polity's deeper waves (the fold's order note, §6). Each domain lights when its own system does: FAITH and ORDER first (the faith contest's local lane and unseating are live; the order stressors are live), health and diplomacy with them (their seams are live), the WILDLAND's run side with MN-2, the CAPTAIN with the generals chain (C4), the SPYMASTER and the DECEIVER with the statecraft band (C1), the MERCHANT PRINCE and the SPECULATOR with EC (C6). **THE FIRST FELT CHAIN:** CH-0 → CH-1 → CH-2 → CH-3's faith rows — the DM introduces a saint, she chooses where to go for her own reasons, she preaches, the faith contest tilts, and the seat may change hands over seasons: "Sister Aude has come to Tolle; the Lantern's faithful grow bolder." … "The Lantern now holds Tolle's high altar." The experience read (requirement 19) asks first: did the DM see her arrive, see why on scrutiny, and see the faith move while she stayed?

| CH-3 | paragonGateRows, paragonLeans | stressorGates 11 · religiousContest 6 · pestilenceKernel 2 · pactFormation 3 · warPeaceDecision 2 | the week after it exerts (health: the same tick) | stressors six presets; per-seam flags | C12 (faith, order, health, diplomacy first) |
| CH-4 | paragonRetainer (the bounty's offer, `setLeader`), paragonRecruit | envoyErrandVocabulary 1 row (the priced-journey consumer) | the offer's card | person tier (the recruiter needs no errand row); `leads` `bodiesEnabled`; the bounty `bodyEffectsEnabled` | C12 (the bounty and `leads` arms with C17) |
| CH-5 | gateDefault (the body party; the attempt on a warden), paragonBans | npcDmVerbs 12 (the restored prior edge; the key's window) | the town's-gate card | belief or truth; a body's turn-back and the attempt `bodiesEnabled` | C12 |
| CH-6 | paragonRelations | 0 | a proximity choice | person regard (RN) | C12 after C11 |
| CH-7 | paragonLiaison, paragonCovert, covertHire (the shared exposure unit, BD its second caller) | RN `TELLING_SOURCES` 2 rows, 0 lines · ES subject kind 1 row | the first accusation | belief; web; ES (C1); a body's tie `bodiesEnabled` | C12 after C1 |
| CH-8 | paragonFailure, Duel, Turn, Arcs | 0 (registry rows) | the hearing card | person tier | C12 |
| CH-9 | (pins, envelopes) | 0 | — | — | C12's read |
| CH-10 | paragonCorps (the corps arm of the record, the stage and the ladder) | WC's free-unit normalizer 3 (WC-10's commit) · WC's DM-authority people source, the stepper's destination input and `removeFreeUnit` (WC-11) — the fold's WC INBOX row (14) | the corps' first retainer card | person tier + WC-10..12 | WC-10's chain (C4) |
| CH-11 | paragonSworn (+ arms in paragonRecord, paragonIntroduce, paragonChoice, gateDefault, paragonMood, and CH-3's faith carrier (3a)) | 0 | the town's-gate line; `keeps_the_faith` on the page | person tier; deity-bearing worlds; presence (any info mode) | C12 after CH-1 · CH-5 |

## §21 THE LIFECYCLE OF EVERY PERSISTED SHAPE (requirement 22a)

**L1 — the `paragon` field** (on `spatialLedgers.npcLedger.{roamers|placed}[wnpcId]`).

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | only the DM's `introduce` (CH1); the engine never mints one |
| read | `paragonOf` only (a source-scan census pins no other dotted reader) |
| persist | inside `normalizeRecord` through the leaf `normalizeParagon`, drop-when-empty at every level, landed in the commit of the first writer (an unknown field is dropped today — executed) |
| regenerate | untouched: the introduced record has an empty `originRef`, so the regen fold's ledger half never matches it (car-0 pin); a settlement it rests in or serves that is rerolled keeps its id |
| undo | the DM's verbs undo through `undoDmVerb` (the introduction's inverse arm inside `npcDmVerbs.js`); the engine's weekly writes undo with the pulse's snapshot restore (THE PROMISE) |
| import | the ledger's normalizer heals garbage to absent ("Total on garbage"): `normalizeParagon` is SHAPE-ONLY and world-less — an unknown vocabulary word drops its sub-field silently, as the pure read door must; the RECEIPT and every id resolution happen in the pass's weekly check, which reads the world |
| migrate | none: absent ⇒ byte-identical (the dormancy golden) |
| dark-after-lit | kept and unread: the stage, the leans and the town's gate no-op; the paragon freezes where it stands; relit, it resumes from its record (a pin) |
| death | the DM's `kill` (`killNamedNpc` → `removeNpcRecord`, and `closeEnvoyErrandsForNpcDeath` closes its errands); the kill's paragon arm first CLOSES an open calling stay and mints its one deed (undoing the kill strikes it); its deeds stay on RN's ledger |
| veil | the DM sees the truth on the paragon page; a court sees what it has heard (RN's belief); the dossier speaks a court's belief in that court's voice |

**L1b — `authored.heldOffice`** (inside L1's authored snapshot): create — ONLY the DM's `introduce` (first or revision) for a BOUND paragon, refused for a second holder of one court's office; read — `securityOfficeOf` only (THE BODIES' office read calls it; the trail arm's office knowledge, §5); persist with L1 (drop-when-absent); regenerate — the employer's settlement id survives a reroll; undo — the DM verb's inverse (the prior snapshot); import — shape-only, a value off the closed list drops; dark-after-lit — kept and unread; the office LAPSES — `securityOfficeOf` reads `null` — the week the bound tie ends (a resignation, the employer's end, a revision to `free`), the field kept as the DM wrote it until the DM revises it; death — with the record (the office then the roster's, BD §9.11); veil — the DM sees it; a court sees its own office-holder; any other court by report. Horizon (requirement 23): one field, bounded by construction.

**L1c — `authored.swornDeity`** (inside L1's authored snapshot; the OWNER's persisted shape, OD-CH-51):

| Path | What happens |
|---|---|
| create | ONLY the DM's `introduce` (first or revision) through `paragonIntroduce.js`, from a DM-authored deity (`validateDeity`; its ref `mintDeityRef(raw)`, the ref a seated town takes) or a god the campaign's world carries (its state key verbatim), built by `commitDeityEmbed`; REQUIRED for a faith champion, whose `creedRef` is written from it; refused with `sworn_creed_conflict` for a faith champion with no oath or sworn elsewhere than its `creedRef`; a LIBRARY paragon's (CH-0b) copied by `commitDeityEmbed` at its introduction; never by the engine |
| read | `swornDeityOf` only — and through it `swornLean`, `swornTemperOf`, §11 step 5b, §7's values term and the faith carrier (3a); a far hearer only as a part of the telling it heard (RN's route) |
| persist | inside `normalizeParagon`'s authored arm: an object with a string `_deityRef`, RE-PICKED through `commitDeityEmbed(embed._deityRef, embed)` (a fixed point — EXECUTED, `cure/D-CH-PD-r1-probe/repick.out.txt`; foreign keys dropped), else dropped whole; an authored faith champion's `creedRef` re-derived from it; drop-when-empty; absent ⇒ the record serializes exactly as before (never a hand-picked key list, which would red the landed walker's one-builder pin) |
| regenerate | untouched — the paragon has no `originRef`, so the regen fold never meets it; a rerolled town that loses the god's creed changes the welcome there, never the field (the paragon's copy is the god as its follower carries it) |
| undo | `introduce`'s inverse in `undoDmVerb` restores the prior authored snapshot, the field with it |
| import | the normalizer heals garbage to absent and re-picks a well-formed embed through the builder; nothing resolves at read (the embed is self-contained) — a god since deleted from the DM's library still leans through the follower's own copy until the DM's revision (§10b) |
| migrate | none — absent = unsworn; no record before CH-11 carries it (a saint introduced before CH-11 stays unsworn until the DM's revision swears it, and its (3a) carries only a seated creed meanwhile) |
| dark-after-lit | kept and unread (the lean, the step and the route no-op); relit, it resumes |
| death | gone with the record at the DM's `kill`; the paragon's deeds stay on RN's ledger; nothing of the god or its creed changes |
| veil | the DM sees it on the paragon page; any other audience sees "sworn to <god>" beside the display noun (the projection arm's non-DM allow-list, §2.4, widened by one entry) unless `secretKind` is `secret_faith` (then DM-only, the projection's paragon arm) |

Requirement-23 horizon: none — one bounded embed per paragon; no ring, no archive.

**L2 — the retainer** (inside L1): create at an `accepted` offer's verdict (or at introduction for a bound paragon); read by the pass and the town's gate (`at_war_with_employer`, `enemy_in_service`); persist with L1; regenerate — the employer id survives a reroll (settlement ids are stable); a removed employer ends the retainer with a receipt; undo — the pulse snapshot; import — shape-only; an employer id that does not resolve is dropped by the pass's weekly check with a receipt; `exposedAt` — written once at exposure and never cleared (the scandal happened; undo is the pulse snapshot); dark-after-lit — kept; no coin moves; death — removed with L1; veil — a covert retainer is the DM's to see; courts see `paragonBeliefs`.

**L3 — habits, moods, the picture, carried facts, conditions, marks, and `aim.exert` · `exertion` · `calling` · `turned`** (inside L1): bounded arrays and banded stocks; create by the pass; decay by `habitCurve` and `bandedStock.decayTowardNeutral`; prune below their epsilon (the sub-field drops); conditions drop at `untilTick`; `marks` are one-way facts (never pruned; `metParty` is written by CH's stage when PT's `met_in_person` deed names the paragon, §16; `curiosityAt` by the curiosity draw; no `turnedAt` and no `crossedAt` mark); `exertion` is overwritten each week the paragon exerts and dropped the week it does not, its `settlementId` written with it (the settlement the exertion served; a `settlementId` that no longer resolves drops the field at the pass's weekly check, with a receipt); `aim.bodyId` is written only with a `hunt_body` aim and dropped with that aim — at the hunt's close, when the DM stops it, or when the body ends (a receipt); `calling` opens as STATE at the first `exerted` week in a town and closes at departure, after a season, or at the DM's kill — and only AT THE CLOSE is its one deed minted through RN's `mintDeed`, append-only, with its magnitude; `soughtBy` rows are created by a court's recruiter and dropped at their `arrivesAt` (an offer card or a "gone" receipt), ≤ 2; `banKeys` hold ONE key per door (a re-ban at that door replaces the key with the newer episode and keeps the stricter window), written with each emergent ban's edge and its `untilTick`; the cap of 8 counts WINDOWED keys only (at a ninth the oldest windowed key evicts, its edge lapsing by its own window); a key whose door stands INDEFINITE is inert, outside the cap, and drops with its edge (a pardon) or once its own `untilTick` passes, surviving an undone `banish` live — whose inverse re-writes its window where still ahead; every key drops when its edge lifts at a seat change, lapses or is pardoned (§11); `postId` is written when a bound paragon placed by the DM's assignment stands outside its lands and cleared by the DM's next assignment home, a resignation or the retainer's end; `turned` is written only by a `polarity_turn` verdict and cleared by striking that verdict or by the DM's `introduce` revision; every other path as L1.

**L3's `aim.roadOpportunity`** (P2; THE OPPORTUNITY's aside mark on a paragon's aim; the OWNER's shape, OD-OP-24 ⭐ VISIBLE): its ten paths are OP §13 L4's, cited and not restated — created only by CH's weekly writer at OP's request, persisted through `normalizeParagon` (absent when no aside was taken on the current aim), restored whole by the pulse's snapshot undo, gone with its aim — CH's writer and normalizer. The `exertion`'s `captive_held` source mark (P2's comment, §4.5) lives and drops with `exertion`.

**L4 — the exclusion edges a paragon carries** (W-H1's, unchanged in shape for persons): create by `introduce` (authored, `indefinite`), `banish` (authored), a `person_banned` verdict (windowed, `untilTick` derived at mint); read first at the town's gate; persist in `npcLedger.exclusions`, strictest-wins merge; regenerate — keyed by settlement ids, which survive; a DELETED settlement's edges must drop (a pin at CH-5, R3 S4 "verify"); undo — `restoreNpcRecord({ exclusions })` restores edges, the DM's verbs their own inverses; import — `normalizeExclusionEdges`; dark-after-lit — kept, unread; death — removed with the record; veil — a court knows its own edicts; others by report.

**L5 — `spatialLedgers.paragonBeliefs`** (CH-7): create by the person stage on a leak telling's ARRIVAL, by a `paragon_employer` confirm/refute product, by a caught leg; read by `believedEmployerOf`, the town's gate, the exposure check; persist conditional, drop-when-empty, ≤ 4 rows per observer (the oldest `vague` row yields); regenerate — keyed by settlement and person ids, both stable; a removed observer's rows drop; undo — the pulse snapshot; import — garbage heals to absent; dark-after-lit — kept, unread; no new row; death of the paragon — its rows drop at the next pass (a pin); a BODY subject's end (THE BODIES' `ended` ring) — its rows drop at the next pass, the same pin (the row's subject may be a body, P3, and every road into a row — a body's leak arrival, a caught courier's receipt, L-BD 47 — is read by the same one writer); death of the employer — its rows drop; veil — each row IS a court's belief, shown only in that court's voice. The ACCUSATION a row's band step writes (§9) lives in the LANDED pair state — `relationshipStates[key]`'s trust, resentment and `recentIncidents` (≤ 8, typed `paragon_leak`) through the one relationship writer — so every lifecycle path is that state's own (persist with the world; undo the pulse snapshot; regenerate keyed by stable settlement ids; dark-after-lit kept, no new incident) and CH declares no new shape for it. Horizon (requirement 23): a row unrefreshed for `a_year` (52 weeks) drops — the belief is forgotten unless the world reminds it.

**L6 — the hearing records** CH's kinds mint: SP-9's PENDING LEDGER and the hearing seal (keyed by engagement id; `engine · tilt · verdict`) carry them — derived, dropped and re-minted like every docket record; the seal survives every path (requirement 24(d)). CH adds no ring of its own.

**L7 — custody:** NO CH SHAPE. A held paragon's custody waits on ES-2b's custody wave (§12.4); the DM's hand is the authored field `authored.heldAt` inside L1 (create · clear: the DM's `introduce` revision only; read: the weekly pass, the page, the town's dossier; persist with L1; regenerate: the settlement id survives a reroll, a deleted settlement drops the field with a receipt at the pass's weekly check; undo: the DM verb's inverse; import: shape-only; dark-after-lit: kept; death: with the record; veil: the DM's own words — any other audience sees "held at <town>").

**L8 — the corps' mark** (on `spatialLedgers.freeUnits[unitKey].paragon`, §16b): create only by the DM's `introduce` with `holderKind: corps` (with WC's DM-authority people SOURCE — the fold's WC INBOX row (14)); read by `paragonOf`'s corps arm; persist inside WC's free-unit normalizer, drop-when-empty, in WC-10's commit; regenerate — WC's free units are worldState lived history, untouched by a settlement reroll; undo — the DM verb's inverse and the pulse snapshot; import — shape-only; dark-after-lit — kept and unread, the unit stands as WC moves it; death — the DM's `disband`, which IS WC's `removeFreeUnit` (WC-11) with its absorb-to-nearest disposition: the mark leaves with the unit record and the people `shed` to the nearest host, conserved; its undo WC's verb's own (PLAUSIBLE until WC-11's car-0); veil — as L1.

**L9 — the two registered tellings' declared content fields** (`content.paragonId`, `content.employerId` and the `what` words `paragon_leak` · `paragon_findings`, on `spatialLedgers.rumorLedgers[observerId][key].content`; RN §3.4: "each declared field is a persisted rumor-ledger field whose lifecycle row is its OWNER's — CH's"): create — only RN's one telling call, from CH's two `TELLING_SOURCES` rows (§8, §9); CH never writes the rumour ledger; read — CH's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE (a leak arrival raises the hearing court's `paragonBeliefs` row, L5) and `reportsBySubject` through the findings' `whereId`/`partyIds` (the home court's belief of the post) — the findings record held by the home court alone (origin the employer's seat, the continuation guard pre-stamped, §8); persist — inside the rumour record, which `degradeTelling` and `mergeArrival` copy whole (`{ ...record.content, partyIds: [...] }`), so the declared fields ride every relay and merge (PLAUSIBLE until CH-7's car-0 pins the save round trip); regenerate — keyed by `wnpc_` and settlement ids, both stable; undo — the pulse snapshot; import — the rumour ledger's own handling; a `paragonId` that resolves to no paragon is skipped by CH's reader with a receipt; dark-after-lit — the rows seed nothing while `paragonCovertEnabled` is dark, and tellings already on the road finish it read by no one; death of the paragon — its tellings finish their road unread (its `paragonBeliefs` rows drop, L5); veil — `visibility: 'mechanical'`, so no rumour panel lists them, and `'spatialLedgers'` is in `worldSnapshotPublic.js :: WORLD_SNAPSHOT_HARD_DENY`, so no public snapshot carries them. Horizon (requirement 23): the rumour ledger's own TTL (`rumorNetwork.js :: RUMOR_TTL_TICKS`, `{ major: 26, notable: 13 }`), never a CH horizon. Draws: the rumour stage forks per record (`rng.fork('rumor-organic:' + eventRef + …)`, `rumorNetwork.js`), so a CH telling moves no other record's draw (PLAUSIBLE: the label-keying is read, not executed); lighting the rows is inside `paragonCovertEnabled`'s declared same-seed shift (OD-CH-20). The findings record's `arrivalTick` (the liaison's priced journey home) is a field of the landed record shape, its life counted from the arrival; a BODY's leak (§9, ONE IDIOM) is THE BODIES' row and lifecycle (its `bodyId`/`bodyKind` slots, BD §2.4), read by CH's one belief writer, never written by CH.

**L10 — `leads`** (inside L1; THE BODIES' item 32): create — ONLY the DM's naming act through CH's leaf `setLeader` and the ledger's one writer, on a NAMED gang or conclave (never a beast; item 32b); read — CH's pass (the leader's mirror, its exertion's place) and THE BODIES' stage through `paragonOf` (the body's choice weights, its cohesion) and THE BODIES' `bodyLeaderOf` (a READ over this field — THE ONE LEADER RECORD; BD stores no copy); write — `setLeader` ONLY, which THE BODIES' `BODY_LEADER` calls, one leader per body (a second naming drops the first's field in the same write); persist with L1; regenerate — a body id is worldState lived history, untouched by a settlement reroll; undo — the naming act's inverse (the prior `leads`, or none) and the pulse snapshot; import — shape-only, and a `bodyId` that resolves to no living body drops the field at the pass's weekly check with a receipt; dark-after-lit — kept and unread (the body's stage reads no leader while CH or THE BODIES is dark); death — the DM's `kill` removes it with the record, and THE BODIES read the leader gone (item 35's split); the body's END drops it the next pass with a receipt; a heard `polarity_turn` drops it (the leader leaves); veil — the DM sees it on the paragon page and the body's page; a court knows a leader only through THE BODIES' belief record of the body ("the leader if known"), never this field. Horizon (requirement 23): one field per paragon, bounded by construction.

**Engine-only, recorded (requirement 14's totality):** the weekly aim, moods, habits, the picture and conditions have no DM verb of their own — engine-only, because the DM's hand on a paragon is WHERE (`assign`), WHAT IT IS (`introduce`'s revision) and WHAT HAPPENS AT EACH ENGAGEMENT (the hearings), and a verb for a mood would let the DM write the paragon's mind past its own nature; the DM may always revise the authored self, which re-weights everything.

## §22 THE SCENARIO MATRIX (each row a pin family at its wave; a MEASURABLE assertion or DARK)

| # | World | What must happen (measurable) |
|---|---|---|
| S1 | all six flags dark, any seed | byte-identical to `81f1cd963` on every preset (the dormancy goldens; `paragonsOf` empty; the stage returns its input by reference) |
| S2 | the DM introduces "Sister Aude", saint of the Lantern, free, fallible, at Kell | the record exists in `roamers` with `paragon.authored`; one authorship receipt; Kell's dossier names her; no other key changed — and every landed ledger reader keeps its declared arm (§2.4): the Wanderers register, the unaffiliates section, the pool census, the roster replacement and the exile read do not see her |
| S3 | the saint supplanting a patron (the owner's example): Aude stays and exerts in Tolle (patron: the Ash; the Lantern holds a foothold) for two seasons | the Lantern's `strengthByRef` in Tolle carries her multiplicand every week she exerts; its share rises above the no-saint control on the same seed; when the patron contest fires the receipt names the tilt; in a larger town with an opposed creed the rise is measurably slower (`targetMass`, `incumbentCounterForce`); S3b — where the Lantern has NO foothold and spread is lit, her exertion notes the Lantern as reaching Tolle (3a) and the creed can enter through `attemptEntry` |
| S4 | the captain who does not help (the owner's example): Captain Vey is in Kell during a siege; free, mercenary, unretained | `aim.exert` is false and no `exertion` is written; no row enters the siege; the `siege` card carries "Captain Vey is in Kell and has not taken up arms"; with a retainer from Kell the same seed exerts |
| S5 | "tired of the north" (the owner's example): Aude has stayed for a year in the northern settlements | on a canon, `wanderlust.awayFrom` names the north (`bearingWord` over world points) and her next move scores southern candidates higher; the beat reads "tired of the north, she has taken the south road"; aspatially it names the DM's region or "these lands"; the reason word `tired_of_region` is on the aim |
| S6 | the handler at the edge (the owner's example): a bound deceiver, employer Merewick, sent by the DM's `assign` to Ashford (outside Merewick's lands) | the pass records `postId: Ashford`; every `RENDEZVOUS_WEEKS` the deceiver walks its OWN leg to Merewick's member nearest Ashford and back; its `carried` facts refresh from Merewick's belief; its findings enter Merewick's beliefs only by the one telling seeded AT MEREWICK'S SEAT with the continuation guard pre-stamped (RN's `TELLING_SOURCES` `findings` row, `eventRef` `rendezvous:<wnpc>:<aim.sinceTick>`, `visibility: 'mechanical'` — on no rumour panel), and no other court's ledger ever holds it; between rendezvous it STAYS at Ashford (`work_here`, posted here) and is never walked home by `return_home`; its re-entry to Ashford is read by Ashford's town's gate (a watch that mistrusts it ⇒ `admitted_under_watch`, ES's wariness up where lit; a refusal ⇒ the caught run, §9 road 3); `worldState.envoyErrands` gains NO row |
| S7 | the clandestine hire exposed (the owner's example): Merewick covertly retains the agitator Hask against Kell (belief preset, web lit) | leak tellings (RN's `TELLING_SOURCES` `leak` row, one week after the exertion, `visibility: 'mechanical'` so no rumour panel lists them, `whereId` empty so no town's belief slot moves) write Kell's `paragonBeliefs` row `vague → uncertain`; at `confident` the stage's blowback call writes `exposedCorruption[Kell>Merewick]`, the edge grievance, `deception` on Merewick's credibility (under `infoStatecraftEnabled`) and `corruption_exposed` on both; the credibility stock is ONE global score, so every court's `reserveFor` reads it lowered; per-court knowledge is each court's `paragonBeliefs` row, and per-court feeling the pair state (one `paragon_leak` incident at each band step, then the blowback's grievance) — never an RN regard of a court; Kell's second step comes only at a fresh arrival, after its first copy has expired (more than 13 weeks after it arrived); a row that stays `confident` fires NO second blowback (`exposedAt`) |
| S8 | the same hire in the omniscient arm | no `paragonBeliefs`, no leak, no accusation, no blowback; the retainer works as a retainer whose employer no court knows — Hask arriving at Kell is NOT refused `enemy_in_service` or `at_war_with_employer` for Merewick's covert service (§11 step 3 reads a covert employer only through a `confident` row, and none exists) (and CH itself is dark in `realistic_regional` until OD-CH-4) |
| S9 | a saint and a heretic whose leans oppose on one creed both exert in Tolle | a `champion_duel` card that week; the default resolves by the two exertions; the loser's exertion is `voided`; three DECIDED duels in a year — three `duel_won` deeds between the pair, either way, on RN's ledger — mark `nemesis`, and an `indecisive` week counts none; a saint of a rival creed and a heretic both working against Tolle's patron do NOT duel |
| S10 | a free doctor, penniless, two plague towns: one paying (a court's offer), one not | a mercenary doctor seeks the paying court; a charitable one goes to the graver plague; the reasons say so |
| S11 | Kell bans the heretic (regard `hatred`) | a `person_banned` card; a `banned` verdict mints a windowed edge with `untilTick` = mint + `a_few_years` and writes `banKeys: { settlementId: Kell, episode: seatEpisodeKeyOf(Kell) }`; the heretic's later arrival is `refused` (`banished_from_here`); ANY seat change in Kell — a coup's `previousGovernments` row, a `seatTransitions` row, or the DM's own tick-less `CHANGE_RULING_POWER` — makes `seatChangedSince(Kell, episode).changed` true and lifts the edge at the next pass, dropping the key; a DM KILL/ASSIGN of Kell's ruler writes neither tail and the card offers the lift; the card names Kell's BENCH officer |
| S12 | the crown of Merewick bans a provocateur; Ashford (a direct member whose believed hold is `shaky`) admits him | Merewick's own town's gate refuses; Ashford's town's gate reads `polity_ban` at `shaky` ⇒ `admitted_under_watch`; with the polity lit (`polityEnabled`; the REQUESTED row under `paragonGateEnabled`) the admission REQUESTS one row through `requestFealtyRow` (episode `polity_ban:p:<wnpc>:<tick>`) and writes it with PL7's deference patch through `applyRelationshipPatch` — ONE `fealty_defied` row, exact-once; the hold moves only through the deference term (`D̄`), never `P`; NO `fealty_break` docket row (PL P40); at `staunch` the same arrival is `refused`; at `frayed` it is admitted with no row |
| S13 | a defiant banished paragon enters Kell in secret | a hidden-path leg; a weekly watch draw; on discovery `banished_found` (`expelled · let_be`, default `expelled`); no `seized` (custody waits on ES-2b's custody wave) |
| S14 | a court hears of a famous doctor while plague is grave at home | a recruiter's PRICED JOURNEY to `believedWhereabouts` — a `soughtBy` row on the doctor's record with its `arrivesAt` from `hopWeeks`; NO `worldState.envoyErrands` row in any world; if she has gone, the row drops with the "gone north" receipt and the court sends no second recruiter for a season; if present, a `retainer_offer` card; two courts in one week share one card; the same with the spine dark (the journey still travels) |
| S15 | a bound magistrate's employer is razed | the retainer ends with a receipt; the paragon is free; its next choice starts from where it stands |
| S16 | a champion's shame holds at its top band for a season and its employers' regard falls to `mistrust` | a `polarity_turn` card; default `holds` unless every condition is at its strongest and the advance is one week; `turns` writes `turned` (no `marks.turnedAt`), `authored.polarity` unchanged; at a month advance the default is `holds`; never silent |
| S17 | a loyal paragon (FIDELITY virtue `defining`, line `never_betray_employer`) gets a princely rival offer | a `never_line_tested` card defaulting `holds_the_line`; crossing (the DM's verdict) writes shame, a drift lesson and a deed |
| S18 | a curious villain hears of the famous party nearby (PT lit) | at most one curiosity `visit_party` per cooldown; never again once met; PT's card defaults "nothing has happened to the party yet" |
| S19 | no map (aspatial) | paragons hop the graph one week a hop; every wave whole except the care lean (spatial canon) |

## §23 THE RULINGS (by delegation under the owner's words of 2026-10-16, 2026-10-17 and 2026-10-20; every row vetoable; ⭐ marks the OWNER's own rows, each VISIBLE)

| Row | The question | Ruling | Ruled |
|---|---|---|---|
| OD-CH-1 | The token | `paragon` in code (module, field, flags); `champion · villain` as polarity VALUES only, with the register note against `'champion-npc'`; the sixteen nouns display-only (R1 rec. 1–3) | 2026-10-17 · by delegation · vetoable |
| OD-CH-2 | The door (persisted shape; owner-gated migration) | CH-0 campaign-scoped `introduce` (a `DM_VERBS` amendment); ⛔ CH-0b the library bucket = a Supabase migration, the OWNER'S HAND, slotted after CH-0 | 2026-10-17 · by delegation · vetoable; CH-0b the OWNER's |
| OD-CH-3 | Requirement 24's scope | EXTENDED by delegation to engagements between a court and a paragon, and between two paragons in a town (the owner's "Same with NPCs in other settlements or with other settlement's NPCs for any act of foreign policy"); a plain admission and a free paragon's broken retainer are not hearings | 2026-10-17 · by delegation · vetoable |
| OD-CH-4 | Lighting | CH carries `npcConsequencesEnabled`: lit with C1's unit in dramatic and full only (OD-FC-39); lighting the person substrate in the DEFAULT preset is a separate signed same-seed shift recorded as an OWNER LIGHTING ROW on the FP-46 roster — every born world changes | 2026-10-17 · amended 2026-10-18 · by delegation · vetoable; the default preset's lighting the OWNER's |
| OD-CH-5 | The mark's home (persisted shape) | one drop-when-empty `paragon` field on the `wnpc_` ledger record, normalized by a leaf in `normalizeRecord`, written only through `moveNpcRecord`; never a roster sub-key, never a new sub-ledger for the person | 2026-10-17 · by delegation · vetoable |
| OD-CH-6 | The weekly pass's host | CH's pass REGISTERS in RN's ONE PERSON STAGE — one pre-return mount in `envoyPulse.js`, RN's; CH adds no host line | 2026-10-17 · amended 2026-10-18 · by delegation · vetoable |
| OD-CH-7 | Habits and drift | the habit curve at person grain with `lawWordFor(lawfulness01)` of the authored alignment's law half (refined on measurement, `cure/L-CH.md` M9); drift through W-LIVES only; the generals design amended by a note at its compile (R1 Q4) | 2026-10-17 · by delegation · vetoable |

## §24 WHAT A DM READS (under the Evidence Law — deeds only, never forecasts; surface unless marked)

- "Sister Aude, a saint of the Lantern, has come to Tolle; the Lantern's faithful grow bolder."
- "Sister Aude has left Kell, tired of the north, and taken the south road." *(on a canon; aspatially "tired of these lands")*
- "Captain Vey is in Kell and has not taken up arms." *(on the siege card)*
- "Merewick's riders reached Tolle to find the doctor gone north."
- "Sister Aude refused Kell's offer: she will not serve a court of the Ash."
- "Kell's Watch Captain shut Kell's gates to the heretic Orrin: word of the burning at Ashford had come before him."
- "Brother Anselm, a healer of renown, tends the sick in Tolle; fewer outbreaks take hold there." *(the plague's receipt, on scrutiny — the paragon named; what care truly does)*
- "In Kell they say Merewick pays the agitator." *(a court's belief, in Kell's voice)*
- "Merewick's hand behind the agitator in Kell is now beyond doubt; Kell's court calls it treachery."
- "Captain Vey and the warlord Hask have met three times now; neither will let it rest."
- "Sister Aude has made a habit of wintering in Tolle." *(the paragon page, on scrutiny)*
- "The Lantern now holds Tolle's high altar."
- "The deceiver Hask is held at Kell." *(the DM's own hand, `authored.heldAt` — the only hold until ES-2b's custody wave)*
- "The Grey Company sold Merewick out at the ford; Kell's banners rode through." *(THE CORPS, §16b)*
- "Kell's watch turned back a stranger on the south road; the trail led to Merewick." *(a posted operative's caught run, §8)*
- "Sister Aude took ship at Tolle for the southern ports; the winter crossing was slow." *(a leg over a sea lane in its storm season — the books' own price, §5)*
- "The Tolle hill gang turned Sister Aude back on the forest path into Ashford's country." *(a body as the road arm's party, §11)*
- "Ilsa, the warden Kell has taken on for its bounty, rides the drovers' way after the Hollow Road gang." *(the hunt led, §4.2; the trail arm, §5)*
- "The agitator Hask now leads the Hollow Road gang." *(the DM's naming, `leads`, §7; a court knows it only once it has heard)*
- "Tolle's silver was found in the Hollow Road gang's den; Kell's court calls it treachery." *(a body's tie exposed by the one idiom, §9 — THE BODIES' sentence for the sponsor)*
- "The Hollow Road gang sent a man to buy Tolle's warden; he sent him back to the hills with nothing." *(the attempt on a warden, `withstood`, §11 — a deed of the gang's)*
- "Bryn has ridden the drovers' ways since Kell made him its warden." *(the office's ways — on scrutiny the paragon page names the office and its appointment)*

- "Kell's Watch Captain let Captain Vey through without a watch: she is sworn to the Lantern, Kell's own faith." *(the creed's step, warmer — §11 step 5b)*
- "Tolle's watch shadowed the warlord Hask while he stayed: he is sworn to the Ash, whose faith has been put out of Tolle's temples." *(the creed's step, colder — a creed is put "OUT OF THE LIGHT by an eviction, a capacity contest or a siege", `religionState.js :: suppressDeity`, never by the court)*
- "Brother Anselm, sworn to the Mother of Wells, has stayed on among Ashford's sick; her faithful there speak his name warmly." *(on scrutiny his page gives `keeps_the_faith`; the faithful's regard is RN's)*
- "Orrin, a fallen saint still sworn to the Lantern, sows doubt in its congregations; the Lantern's faithful have turned against him." *(the fallen one — the flock's regard and his shame, §10b)*
Every line names who, which power, and what happened; none names a number, a mechanism or what might happen next; in the sworn lines the followers act, and nothing is said of a god.
